For Pegatron, we expect 4Q26 revenue to reach NT$514bn (+32% QoQ, +61% YoY), as 
we expect to see more contribution from AI server going into 4Q26. We expect GM to 
slightly decrease to 4.2% (-0.3ppt QoQ and +0.1%ppt YoY). We continue to see 4Q26 
revenue with QoQ growth on a continuing ramp-up in AI server shipments as well as the 
continued peak season for its communication business. 
For Compal, we expect 4Q26 revenue to reach NT$282bn (+16% QoQ, +48% YoY) with 
increasing contribution from AI server  as the company continue to expand from 
motherboard (L6) to system level (L10/L11). We expect GM to slightly increase to 4.6% 
(+0.2ppt QoQ and -1.2ppt YoY) as the YoY contraction is due to shift in product mix (we 
expect PC business to account for 60% in 4Q26 vs. 71% in 4Q25).  
For Asus, we expect 4Q26 revenue to reach NT$283bn (+2% QoQ, +40% YoY), with solid 
growth from its server business, offsetting the weakness from its PC business, although 
we continue to expect a more premium PC product mix. We expect GM to decrease to 
14.8% (-1.6ppt QoQ and -1.1ppt YoY) due to the change in product mix.

### Page 3

Earnings revisions 
Quanta 
We revise down our 2026 earnings estimates by 3% to factor in 3Q26 results (we revise 
up our 2026E revenue estimates by 2%). We revise down 2027/28E earnings estimates 
by 7%/1% to reflect a product mix with higher contribution from the server business (we 
revise up our 2027/28E revenue estimates by 1/2%). We now expect the GM to contract 
going forward due to the higher server business contribution, thus revise down GPM by 
0.3/0.2/0.1ppt in 2026/27/28E.

Pegatron 
We revise down our 2026 earnings estimates by 20% to factor in 3Q26 results (we 
maintain our 2026E revenue estimates). We revise down 2027 earnings estimates by 5% 
to reflect a higher % for minority interest while revise up 2028 earnings estimates by 3% 
with improved GM (we revise down our 2027/28E revenue estimates by 9/6% to reflect 
a more conservative assumption for its communication business). We now expect the 
GM to remain stable going forward, thus revise up GPM by 0.4/0.7/0.5ppt in 
2026/27/28E.

### Page 4

Compal 
We revise down our 2026 earnings estimates by 10% to factor in 3Q26 results (our 
2026E revenue estimates change immaterially). We revise down 2027/28E earnings 
estimates by 4/7% to factor in the impact from minority interest (we revise up our 
2027/28E revenue estimates by 9/9% to reflect a more constructive assumption on its 
server business). We now expect the GM to remain stable going forward, thus only revise 
down 0.1ppt in 2026E.

Asus 
We revise up our 2026 earnings estimates by 11% to factor in 3Q26 results (we revise up 
our 2026E revenue estimates by 2%). We revise down 2027/28E earnings estimates by 
11/15% to factor in the impact from minority interest (we revise up our 2027/28E 
revenue estimates by 3/4% to reflect a higher contribution from its server business). We 
now expect the GM to decline going forward with higher server contribution, thus revise 
down 0.5/0.8ppt in 2027/28E.

Valuation 
Quanta 
Maintain Neutral on Quanta, with a new TP of NT$280 (from NT$300), based on the 
same 10.7x 2027E EPS (which is the company’s 2018-23 average P/E), and factoring in 
our latest earnings revisions.

Pegatron 
Maintain Sell on Pegatron, with a new TP of NT$71 (from NT$73.3), based on the same 
9.7x 2027E EPS (which is the company’s 2018-23 average P/E), and factoring in our 
latest earnings revisions.

Compal 
Maintain Neutral on Compal, with a new TP of NT$45 (from NT$46.1), based on the 
same 14.7x 2027E EPS (which is +0.5 STDV above the company’s 2018-23 average P/E), 
and factoring in our latest earnings revisions.

### Page 6

Asus 
Maintain Neutral on Asus, with a new TP of NT$1,000 (from NT$1,115), based on the 
same 14.8x 2027E EPS (which is +0.5 STDV above the company’s 2018-23 average P/E), 
and factoring in our latest earnings revisions.

Investment Thesis & PT methodology and risks

Investment Thesis - Quanta 
Quanta is an ODM company focusing on the design and manufacturing of PCs, tablets, 
consumer electronics, servers, etc. We are Neutral rated on Quanta. We expect the 
company’s earnings to grow in 2026-28E, driven by 1) growing AI server demand from 
Quanta’s CSP customers, and 2) general server demand recovery. However, we see P/E 
valuation as fair at the current level, given the positives appear to have been mostly 
factored in the share price and the current upside to our target price is relatively lower 
compared to its peers under coverage. We would consider becoming more positive if the 
AI server demand from its CSP clients turn out to be above our expectations.

### Page 7

Price Target Risks and Methodology - Quanta 
We are Neutral rated on Quanta. Our 12-month target price of NT$280 is based on a 
10.7x 2027E P/E, which is the company’s 2018-23 average P/E.  
Key upside/downside risks include: 1) stronger-/weaker-than-expected PC market 
recovery; 2) faster-/slower-than-expected AI server ramp-up; and 3) 
stronger-/weaker-than-expected demand for general servers. 
Pegatron is an ODM company focusing on the design and manufacturing of 
smartphones, PCs, consumer electronics, and servers, etc. While we like Pegatron’s 
product line diversification into servers, AI servers, and automotive electronics, we are 
Sell rated as we see revenue contribution from AI server remaining small in near-term, 
and our 2027E target P/E is near the company’s current trading P/E. We expect 
smartphone business will remain the major revenue contributor from 2026E to 2028E, 
and we see the AI server related revenue contribution remains 4% in 2026E (vs. peers 
14%~87%). Catalysts for further upside will be its faster-than-expected progress in AI 
servers. 
Price Target Risks and Methodology - Pegatron 
We are Sell rated on Pegatron with a 12-month target price of NT$71. Our TP is based 
on a 9.7x 2027E P/E, which is the company’s 2018-23 average P/E. 
Key upside risks: 1) stronger-than-expected smartphone market recovery, 2) 
faster-than-expected AI server ramp-up, and 3) stronger-than-expected demand on 
consumer electronics.

Investment thesis- Compal 
Compal is an ODM company focusing on the design and manufacturing of PCs, tablets, 
consumer electronics, servers, etc. While we like Compal’s solid position in the PC ODM 
market and its expansion into AI servers, we are Neutral rated as we see the contribution 
from AI servers as limited in the near-term given its limited exposure to cloud server 
customers, who are the early adopters of AI servers. Meanwhile, we believe valuation 
already reflects near-term positives. We model Compal earnings growth in 2026-26E to 
be mainly driven by commercial PC demand recovery and server business ramping up. 
We see P/E valuation as fair at current levels compared to peers given its mild earnings 
growth in 2026-28E.

Price Target Risks and Methodology - Compal 
We are Neutral-rated on Compal with a 12-month target price of NT$45. Our target 
price is based on 14.7x 2027E P/E, which is +0.5 STDV above the company’s 2018-23 
average P/E. 
Key upside/downside risks: 1) stronger-/weaker-than-expected PC market recovery; 
2) faster-/slower-than-expected AI server ramp-up; and 3) 
stronger/weaker-than-expected demand for tablets.

### Page 8

Investment thesis- Asus 
Asus is a multinational computer hardware and electronics company based in Taiwan 
and is the top motherboard and gaming brand globally and also a top-three consumer 
notebook vendor, per the company. We are Neutral rated on Asus considering (1) 
challenging PC market under end of Win10 replacement cycle, rising memory costs, and 
consumption pull in in 1H25 under China government subsidies and tariff concerns, (2) 
ASUS brand AI servers remain small in shipment. The stock currently trades at 14x 2027E 
PE, close to our target PE multiple (14.8x), which we think is fair.  
We would turn more positive on ASUS if we see (1) its PC revenue growth stronger than 
expected which could be driven by faster than expected product mix upgrade toward AI 
PC or gaming PC, or (2) faster than expected AI servers shipment ramp up, which could 
be driven by penetration in leading enterprises or CSPs (cloud services providers).

Price Target Risks and Methodology - Asus 
We are Neutral-rated on ASUS with a 12-month target price of NT$1,000. Our target 
price is based on 14.8x 2027E P/E, which is +0.5 STDV above the company’s 2018-23 
average P/E 
Key upside/downside risks include: 1) stronger / weaker-than-expected PC market 
growth; 2) faster / slower-than-expected AI and gaming PC growth; and 3) faster / 
slower-than-expected AI server ramp-up.

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.
Coverage Universe: A list of all stocks in each coverage universe is available by primary analyst, stock and coverage universe at

https://www.gs.com/research/hedge.html.
Not Rated (NR). The investment rating, target price and earnings estimates (where relevant) are removed pursuant to Goldman Sachs policy when
Goldman Sachs is acting in an advisory capacity in a merger or in a strategic transaction involving this company, when there are legal, regulatory or
policy constraints due to Goldman Sachs’ involvement in a transaction, and in certain other circumstances. Early-Stage Biotech (ES). An investment

rating and a target price are not assigned pursuant to Goldman Sachs policy when this company has neither a drug, treatment or medical device that
has passed a Phase II clinical trial nor a license to distribute a post-Phase II drug, treatment or medical device.  Rating Suspended (RS). Goldman Sachs
Research has suspended the investment rating and price target for this stock, because there is not a sufficient fundamental basis for determining an
investment rating or target price. The previous investment rating and target price, if any, are no longer in effect for this stock and should not be relied

upon.  Coverage Suspended (CS). Goldman Sachs has suspended coverage of this company.  Not Covered (NC). Goldman Sachs does not cover this
company.

## Document 138: 261117_ubs_BIS-control

Source PDF: all report pdf/261117_ubs_BIS-control.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-11-17
Pages: 12
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

ab 17 November 2026
Global Research
UBS Global I/O Semiconductors
New US Export Controls on Cloud Access;
Assessing the Impact
Summary Thoughts
The US Dept. of Commerce Bureau of Industry and Security (BIS) posted a new rule
addressing remote access to advanced semiconductors. Following discussions with
industry experts (including a former Commerce Dept. official), we believe the rule  is
essentially attempting to accomplish the same thing as the AI Diffusion Rule issued by

the Biden Administration, but never put into effect. We would note that the rule was
"written by, and at the direction of the  Department of Treasury" - atypical for such a
rule, according to our experts. While we can't say for sure that this is in direct response
to the NVDA situation, the timing seems highly coincidental and we highlighted this risk
here and in our estimate cuts yesterday morning. We would make a few key points:
 Demand impact is hard to assess, but could be significant. Current export

control laws have not required a license  for shipments to key countries such as
Malaysia, Thailand, Indonesia, and Singapore (though we note  there is little room
for data centers in Singapore). As a result, we have seen a large increase in data
center construction in Malaysia in particular with recent press reports from the
NYT and Economist  (here and here) suggesting ~2GW of new data centers now
online in a single province in Malaysia with another ~10GW currently under

construction, and  total approved volume up to ~25GW with backing from both US
and China hyperscalers. On this basis, it seems fair to assume  a  run-rate for
shipments into Malaysia alone of a few GW/yr and the NYT article indicated that
about half of this is going to China-domiciled  companies. There is a carve-out in
the new rule for online retailers and social media companies but we would think
that US companies would err on the side of conservatism unless they can be

continuously assured of the end customer. Ergo, it is possible that this new rule
could strip 1-2GW out of current demand, with most of this likely being from
NVDA. At $30B+/GW, this is roughly consistent with our estimate of a 10%
demand impact, though NVDA should still be able to divert these shipments to
other customers to mitigate any near-term impact so we see this as more of a
longer-term issue especially if powered shell permitting gets more restrictive in the

US.
 Odds of MATCH Act passage have gone up, but timing remains a key Q. We
have written in the past about  MATCH  here and here. While the US may not want
to enforce additional export controls on the heels of this new cloud access rule,
our conversations with experts marked a clear shift in tone around the odds of the
MATCH Act passing vs. prior months - with the legislation potentially being
attached to the NDAA in this upcoming Congress. That being said, an expert firmly

believes that the disparity between what ASML, TEL, and other foreign equipment
suppliers currently sell into China vs. US SPE suppliers is not all that significant,
making the rule more of a point of tension with US allies such as the Netherlands
and Japan. Second, the goal of this legislation would be "level the playing field"
using the FDPR (Foreign Direct Product Rule) but there is a question of who would
determine which tools sold by the Dutch and Japanese suppliers are “equivalent”

to those currently restricted for US equipment suppliers. Third, a contemplated
ban on immersion DUV would make supporting trailing-edge fabs and lines in
China quite challenging, with potential spillover effects across the industry, even
outside China. Fourth, it raises the question of what incentives, if any, the Dutch
and Japanese governments would have to comply. Related to this, an expert
indicated that the use of FDPR by the USG could be more upsetting than tariffs

### Page 2

directly impacted by this rule, but in our coverage, we see KLAC being the only one
really indirectly negatively affected by the passage of this rule given its high attach
to litho process steps (and others affected to a lesser extent, though less litho
could in fact ripple to fewer dep/etch tools).
Read Across
We see NVDA as the most directly impacted by this rule, though the entire supply chain,
from memory through SPE, would ultimately be negatively affected by the closure of

such a large demand corridor. That said, we could argue that, over the longer term,
approving  the MATCH Act could benefit memory suppliers outside China by
constraining domestic Chinese capacity additions.

Investing in structured investments involves significant risks. For a detailed discussion of the risks involved in investing in any particular structured investment, you must read

the relevant offering materials for that investment. Structured investments are unsecured obligations of a particular issuer with returns linked to the performance of an
underlying asset. Depending on the terms of the investment, investors could lose all or a substantial portion of their investment based on the performance of the underlying
asset. Investors could also lose their entire investment if the issuer becomes insolvent. UBS does not guarantee in any way the obligations or the financial condition of any

issuer or the accuracy of any financial information provided by any issuer. Structured investments are not traditional investments and investing in a structured investment is
not equivalent to investing directly in the underlying asset. Structured investments may have limited or no liquidity, and investors should be prepared to hold their investment
to maturity. The return of structured investments may be limited by a maximum gain, participation rate or other feature. Structured investments may include call features and,

if a structured investment is called early, investors would not earn any further return and may not be able to reinvest in similar investments with similar terms. Structured
investments include costs and fees which are generally embedded in the price of the investment. The tax treatment of a structured investment may be complex and may differ
from a direct investment in the underlying asset. UBS and its employees do not provide tax advice. Investors should consult their own tax advisor about their own tax situation

before investing in any securities.

Update
MediaTek  | Asia Pacific
Asia Pacific Summit 2026
Feedback
Potential to win next gen TPU projects, while other ASIC
customers remain undisclosed.
1. Major AI ASIC project (TPU) - potential to win next generation projects.
• TPU v8 shipment track, US$2.5bn revenue in 4Q26. Reiterated US$16bn
revenue in 2027, high end of guidance. As there are more customers than
chip supply, if ABF substrate supply improves, revenue can improve.
• TPU v9 EMIB-T substrate supply is also key, company is comfortable on yield

and output trajectory.
• It sees its TPU v9 project as a major TPU SKUs for the customer. MediaTek
revenue per chip is higher than it was in v8. The compute die is handled by
the customer.
• MediaTek said it is possible for the customer to use designed TPU (eg. v8t
and v9) for AI LLM customer's rack system, besides Google's internal use.
• On third generation (TPU v10), MediaTek is working with customer's internal
team, and has high confidence to win the project.

• New generation TPU compute die handled by customer. MediaTek will focus
on D2D inference, I/O die, and chip packaging technology.
• We maintain our view that Alchip is strong candidate for TPU compute die
backend design. Based on Alchip’s NRE revenue timing, we think the
compute die could be for the TPU v10.
2. Other ASIC customers - awaiting more disclosure: MediaTek still cannot
disclose the non-China CSP customer name or project details. The engagement with

China CSP is ongoing, but needs to comply with US BIS rules.
3. ASIC business financial profile - OP margin improvement: Mediatek said a major
customer wanted to drive to full COT (customer own tool), but with the chip's
complexity growing, it hopes to sustain ASIC margin in future generations. The
company reiterated its 3Q earning call comments that ASIC op. margin is 10% above
the previous average. It reiterated its op margin can improve to 20%+ in 2027 on

higher ASIC revenue contribution. We estimate ASIC op. margin at 25-30%.
4. Smartphone business - still challenging: High memory costs continue to impact
China demand. Blended ASP for MediaTek's SoC has risen on price hikes and better
product mix.
Reiterate OW & Top Pick: MediaTek continues to win new TPU projects with higher
revenue per chip, and so revenue growth may extend to 2030-31. The chance to
supply to AI LLM for end-customer's rack system via a major customer could also

the 2027 Extel Asia Research Survey.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
MediaTek (2454.TW)
Base case, residual income model. Key assumptions: cost of equity 9.2%, intermediate growth
rate 12.0%, terminal growth rate 3.0%.
Risks to Upside
n Edge AI proliferates smartphone replacement cycle.
n Smartphone demand rises in China and other EMs.
n New products attract high demand, resulting in market share gains.
n Google's TPU spurs demand.
Risks to Downside

n Smartphone demand deteriorates in China and other EMs.
n Competition heats up, resulting in pricing competition.
n New products attract low demand, causing market share loss.
n Margin dilution is more severe.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Company feedback suggests the fundamental outlook for 2027
could be better than feared, especially for top-tier companies in
the AI hardware supply chain. Consumer electronics companies
are passing through the worst of the downturn, with a potential
recovery likely in 2027.

Greater China Technology Hardware

Asia Pacific
Industry View In-Line
Recent Research:                  Global Technology and
Telecom: 2026 AlphaWise Smartphone Survey:
Another Down Year That's Not Yet Priced In (16
Nov 2026)
While we believe some investors are turning more bearish on AI amid top-down
uncertainty, bottom-up discussions with supply chain companies suggest the
outlook for 2027 is better than feared. Within the AI universe, our key
recommendations include: Innolight, Largan, Wistron, Hon Hai, Wiwynn, Unimicron,

EMC, Lenovo, Delta, Bizlink and Huaqin.
For non-AI-driven consumer electronics companies, we believe any slower memory
price increases could provide a positive catalyst. Companies under our coverage
supplying components to Apple appear better positioned to deliver growth in 2027,
in our view, while some Android suppliers we spoke with anticipate potentially
weaker volumes. Our key recommendations include Xiaomi, Luxshare-H, Crystal and
Transsion.

AI servers, optical interconnects and infrastructure
- Hon Hai – AI demand remains strong; additional rack assembly share expected.
Management reiterated 30%+ AI demand growth in 2027, with no recent
deterioration. ASICs should remain approximately 20% of cloud revenue, as higher
VR200 rack prices offset faster ASIC growth. Hon Hai expects to gain rack assembly
share through the addition of one hyperscaler and one neocloud customer.
- Zhongji Innolight – Most 2027 contracts have confirmed volumes and prices, with

some customers providing 2028 guidance. Customers have increased 1.6T orders,
partly offset by lower 800G orders, with no backlog cancellations. NPO demand
has strengthened; volume production is scheduled for 2H27, with stronger growth in
2028. Annual transceiver price reductions remain normal, but silicon photonics,
product upgrades and a more efficient one-laser-to-four-lanes solution should
support margins.
Continued on the next page.
Morgan Stanley does and seeks to do business with

communications with a subject company, public appearances
and trading securities held by a research analyst account.
Key Takeaways
Innolight, Largan, Wistron, Hon Hai, Wiwynn, Unimicron, Lenovo, Delta, Bizlink
and Huaqin are our preferred stocks for the AI theme.
We believe Xiaomi, Luxshare-H, Crystal and Transsion are likely to enjoy a
gradual recovery over the following quarters.
November 22, 2026 11:03 PM GMT

### Page 3

M
Update
Morgan Stanley Research 3
Summary of Our Summit Feedback and Links to Individual
Company Reports
- Quanta – Improving AI product mix supports profitability; notebooks remain weak. Non-
NVIDIA projects should contribute a double-digit percentage of AI server revenue in 2027,
up from single digits in 2026, and generally carry higher margins. Quanta is negotiating
additional GPU consignment arrangements, although most remain unfinalized. Neocloud

exposure is limited to a single-digit percentage of total revenue.
- Huaqin – China SuperPoD deployments offer a multi-year growth opportunity. Volume
deliveries to two major Chinese cloud service providers began in 2H26 and should expand
to three in 2027. Huaqin has completed development of a 3.2T NPO system solution, with
a 6.4T solution under development, and plans to begin customer promotion in 2027.
- Bizlink – The company believes >20% sequential revenue growth in 4Q26 is achievable,

supported by organic growth and Interplex. Power interconnect dollar content could
double on the next GPU platform, alongside market share gains; separate power racks
offer further potential depending on customer deployment. Optical diversification
includes fiber patch cords, active optical cables from 2H27, and CPO initiatives.
- Delta Electronics – Management sees potential for double-digit sequential revenue
growth in 4Q26 and stronger-than-seasonal performance in 1Q27. Component tightness is

likely to ease from 2Q27. High-voltage direct-current power rack shipments should start in
4Q26, initially with limited revenue contribution. Delta cited 80VDC as the current
reference design for the 2H27 GPU platform, although the design remains unfinalized.
- Lite-On – Management expects revenue growth of 5–10% QoQ in 4Q26, followed by
another single-digit increase in 1Q27, and reiterated its 70% cloud revenue growth target
for 2027. Power supplies, battery backup units and power racks should benefit from rising

AI cluster power consumption. Normalized gross/operating margin targets are 25–
30%/13–15%. Shipments of 800VDC power racks should begin in 3Q27.
Non-AI-driven consumer electronics
- Luxshare – Consumer electronics share gains, new AI devices and PC ODM development
offer growth opportunities. Luxshare also targets large-scale optical transceiver
production in 2027, complementing its electrical interconnect, liquid cooling and AI power
offerings.
- Sunny Optical – Sunny aims to sustain handset lens revenue despite industry weakness

and gain module share at Samsung and Huawei. Potential new product launches for a key
customer could support strong XR growth in 2027, while robotics offers a multi-year
optical demand opportunity. Passive optical interconnect components are undergoing
customer qualification.
- Transsion – 3Q26 is likely to mark the earnings trough; management targets positive
revenue and earnings growth in 2027. Memory inventories are normalizing, and
management considers further substantial memory price increases less likely. Higher

processor costs should have a relatively limited impact on the overall bill of materials.
Management targets energy storage revenue of Rmb1.5–2.0 billion in 2026 and more than

### Page 4

M
Update
4
double that amount in 2027.

ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

精測(6510 TT) Buy

精測受惠 HPC 測試需求，2027 年 營 收、獲 利 皆 可 望 再 創 新
高 ， 給 Buy 的 投 資 評 等 。  
投 資 建 議：精 測 受 惠 於 HPC(TPU、AI5 等 )測 試 需 求，2027 年營
收 、 獲 利 皆 可 望 再 創 新 高 ，給 Buy 的 投 資 評 等 。  
精測 3Q26 營 收 、 獲 利 皆 創 單 季 新 高 ： 精測 3Q26 營收 19.05 億
元，QoQ+16.17%\YoY+53.35%；毛 利 率 57.26%、營 益 率 35.21%；
稅後純益 5.51 億 元，QoQ+11.61%\YoY+100.03%；稅 後 EPS 16.76
元。3Q26 營 收 、 獲 利 皆 創 單 季 新 高 ， 雖 然 因 新 產 品 開 始 認 列 營
收 ， 毛 利 率 略 為 季 減 ， 但 獲 利 成 長 還 是 大 於 營 收 成 長 。  
精 測 有 TPU 與 AI5 商機： 精 測 表 示 ， 美 系 客 戶 的 TPU 供 應 商
維 持 既 有 架 構 ， 預 期 在  TPU8 專 案 中 將 扮 演 一 定 角 色 ， 不 過 目
前 尚 未 正 式 發 包，相 關 專 案 預 計 於 2H26 啟 動。此 外，針 對 另 一
客戶的 AI5 專 案， 依 客 戶 規 劃 時 程， 預 計 06/2026 進 行 工 程 卡
交 付 ， 並 預 計 於 2026 年底至 2027 年 初 進 入 量 產 。  
精 測 新 產 能 持 續 開 出：2026 年 營 運 逐 季 成 長 趨 勢 明 確，3Q26 營
收 有 機 會 雙 位 數 季 增 ， 08/2026 、 03/2027 會 有 比 較 大 的 產 能 開
出 。 目 前 交 期 約 5~8 周 ， 新 產 能 客 戶 驗 證 時 間 約 需 一 季 ， 2027
年 營 運 健 康 成 長 。

個股報告  
                                        2026 年 11 月 24 日

2 群益投顧 
  ESG：  
在 環 境 保 護 方 面 ， 中 華 精 測 於 2022 年 開 始 自 主 性 導 入 溫 室 氣 體 盤 查 作 業 ，
除 了 計 算 碳 使 用 總 量 外，也 了 解 如 何 節 能 及 尋 找 替 代 能 源，並 推 動 各 項 環 保
節 能 減 碳 措 施、減 少 溫 室 氣 體 排 放 量 等，以 期 減 緩 與 調 適 氣 候 變 遷 所 帶 來 之
營 運 衝 擊 。  
在 社 會 責 任 方 面 ， 中 華 精 測 遵 守 相 關 法 規 及 國 際 人 權 公 約 ， 持 續 營 造 友 善 、
平 等、自 由 的 工 作 環 境，對 於 提 升 員 工 福 祉 不 遺 餘 力，且 不 定 期 舉 辦 家 庭 活
動 及 獎 勵 同 仁 結 婚 、 生 育 、 旅 遊 津 貼 等 員 工 福 利 。  
在 公 司 治 理 營 運 績 效 方 面 ， 中 華 精 測 連 續 兩 年 於 公 司 治 理 評 鑑 排 名 前 5%，
並於 2022 年 榮 獲 上 櫃 公 司 治 理 評 鑑  Top5%之 佳 績，秉 持 誠 信 正 直 原 則，定
期 宣 導 及 教 育 訓 練 內 線 交 易、誠 信 廉 潔 等 法 律 知 識，確 保 公 司 員 工 遵 守 誠 信
經 營 守 則 之 落 實 。  
公 司 簡 介 ：  
中 華 精 測 科 技 股 份 有 限 公 司 (6510 TT)設 立 於 08/2005，總 部 及 生 產 工 廠 皆 位
於 桃 園 平 鎮，為 中 華 電 信 所 屬 關 係 企 業，前 身 為 中 華 電 信 研 究 所 內 部 之 高 速
PCB 團 隊 ，2001 年 起 開 始 嘗 試 研 發 設 計 測 試 載 板 (Load board) 及 探 針 卡 載 板
(Probe PCB) 。  
中 華 精 測 主 要 從 事 晶 圓 測 試 板 、 IC 測 試 板 、 技 術 服 務 與 其 他 等 業 務 ， 提 供
半導體產業測試所需的介面板服務，用於晶圓測試之探針卡專用印刷電路
板 (Probe PCB) ， 及 用 於 IC 測 試 之 載 版 (Load Board) 。  
中 華 電 信 (2412 TT) 持股中 華 投 資 公 司 約 89.0% ； 中 華 投 資 持 股 精 測 約
33.6%；聯發科(2454 TT)於 2015 年，透 過 旗 下 翔 發 投 資 公 司 入 股 中 華 精 測，
目 前 持 股 約 0.5%。  
精 測 的 台 灣 同 業 包 含 旺 矽 (6223 TT)、穎崴(6515 TT)、 雍 智 科 技 (6683 TT)…
等。精 測 的 客 戶 包 含 美 系 HPC 公 司、台 系 IC 設 計 公 司、美 系 IC 設 計 公 司，
台 系 晶 圓 代 工 廠 …等。 
營 收 占 比 _產 品 別 ：  
產 品 目 前 毛 利 率 排 序 ： 整 組 探 針 卡 >FT 用的 loadboard>SLT 板 / 老化板。 
晶 圓 測 試 卡 包 含 PCB 與 整 組 探 針 卡 。

3 群益投顧 
資 料 來 源 ： 精 測 法 說 會 簡 報 。  
營 收 占 比 _產 品 應 用 別 ：

資 料 來 源 ： 精 測 法 說 會 簡 報 。  
精測 4Q26 毛 利 率 可 能 季 減 ：  
精 測 之 前 說 3Q26 毛 利 率 區 間 為 50~55%，但 實 際 毛 利 率 優 於 預 期，因 09/2026
才 開 始 出 毛 利 率 較 差 的 老 化 板，所 以 對 3Q26 毛 利 率 影 響 有 限，精 測 估 4Q26
毛利率 50~55%， 但 還 是 要 看 產 品 組 合 。  
精 測 估 2027 年 營 收 、 獲 利 雙 位 數 成 長 ：  
精測估 1Q27 營 收 將 介 於 3Q26~4Q26 間，精 測 表 示 以 目 前 訂 單 的 狀 況，2027
年 營 收 維 持 50%的 成 長 ， EPS 的 成 長 也 是 可 以 期 待 。  
整 組 探 針 卡 於 往 年 的 合 併 營 收 占 比 約 20~30%，精 測 2027 年 挑 戰 占 比 30%，
但 也 要 看 其 他 產 品 的 成 長 性 。  
現 在 由 於 營 收 規 模 成 長，精 測 建 議 費 用 率 預 估 為 20~25%，用 於 研 發、留 才、
與 國 際 大 廠 的 合 作 等 。  
TPU 工 程 驗 證 產 品 預 計 11/2026 開 始 出 貨 ：  
傳聞 TPU 產 品 因 封 裝 問 題 ， 量 產 時 間 遞 延 ， 精 測 表 示 工 程 驗 證 產 品 的 出 貨
沒 有 遞 延 ， 量 產 時 間 預 計 會 是 1H27， 量 產 的 數 量 還 沒 有 確 定 。  
TPU 專 案，精 測 將 於 11/2026 開 始 出 貨 工 程 驗 證 用 的 整 組 探 針 卡，之 後 量 產
的 數 量 還 沒 確 定 。 記 憶 體 用 的 整 組 探 針 卡 也 會 於 2026 年 送 樣 。  
美 系 車 用 晶 片 大 廠 的 訂 單 ， 精 測 也 於 2026 年 開 始 出 貨 工 程 驗 證 的 產 品 ， 預
計將於 2027 年 量 產 ， 之 後 量 產 的 數 量 還 沒 確 定 。 工 程 驗 證 結 束 ， 到 正 式 量
產 通 常 需 要 1~2 季。 
除了 TPU 專 案 以 外 ， 精 測 也 積 極 驗 證 北 美 、 韓 國 客 戶 ， 精 測 估 2027 年 的 產
能 與 稼 動 率 皆 能 維 持 高 檔 。  
精 測 有 發 CB， 三 廠 產 能 預估 4Q28 開出：  
精測 07/29 重 訊 決 議 發 行 CB， 預 計 募 資 20 億 元 ， 主 要 用 於 新 廠 的 工 程 款 ，

8 群益投顧 
  【 投 資 評 等 說 明 】  
  評等 定義 
  強力買進(Strong Buy)  首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間 ≥ 35%  
  買進(Buy)  15%≤首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間 <35%  
  區間操作(Trading Buy) 5%≤首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間 <15%

中立(Neutral)  
無 法 由 基 本 面 給 予 投 資 評 等  
預 期 近 期 股 價 將 處 於 盤 整  
建 議 降 低 持 股

【 投 資 評 等 調 整 機 制 】  
  投 資 評 等 係「 首 次 」給 予 特 定 個 股 投 資 評 等 時，其 前 一 日 收 盤 價 相 較 3 個月
目 標 價 之 潛 在 上 漲 空 間 計 算 而 得 。 個 股 投 資 評 等 分 為 四 個 等 級 ， 定 義 如 上 。
爾 後 的 投 資 評 等 係 依 循 「 首 次 評 等 」 ， 直 到 停 止 推 薦 。   
「 強 力 買 進 」、「 買 進 」 及 「 區 間 操 作 」 均有 upside 目 標 價 。 差 別 僅 在 於 ，
首 次 評 等 時 潛 在 報 酬 率 不 同 。 「 中 立 」 則 無 目 標 價 。  
一 旦 我 們 給 予 特 定 個 股「 強 力 買 進 」、「 買 進 」或「 區 間 操 作 」之 投 資 評 等，
就 是 責 任 的 開 始，爾 後 將 透 過 各 式 研 究 報 告 作 定 期 性、持 續 性 追 蹤 基 本 面 及
股 價 變 化 ， 直 到 停 止 推 薦 。  
停 止 推 薦 情 境 ：  
1. 達 目 標 價 。  
2. 雖 未 達 原 訂 目 標 價 ， 但 檢 視 基 本 面 、 訊 息 面 、 籌 碼 面 等 多 方 訊 息 ， 研
判 股 價 上 漲 空 間 已 然 有 限 ， 將 適 時 出 具 降 評 報 告 。  
3. 推 薦 後 股 價 不 漲 反 跌 ， 亦 將 出 具 降 評 報 告 。

【免責聲明】 
  本 研 究 報 告 僅 提 供 予 特 定 人 之 客 戶 作 為 參 考 資 料「 非 經 同 意 不 得 轉 載 」。我
們 並 不 確 保 此 資 訊 的 完 整 性 與 正 確 性，投 資 人 應 了 解，報 告 中 有 關 未 來 預 測
之 陳 述 可 能 不 會 實 現，因 而 不 應 被 依 賴。而 且 此 報 告 並 非 根 據 特 定 投 資 目 的
或 依 預 定 對 象 之 財 務 狀 況 所 撰 寫 出 來 的，因 此，此 研 究 報 告 的 目 的，既 非 對
投 資 人 於 買 賣 證 券、選 擇 權、期 貨 或 其 他 證 券 相 關 之 衍 生 性 商 品 提 供 詢 價 服
務，亦 非 作 為 進 行 交 易 的 要 約。投 資 人 應 注 意 到 相 關 證 券 之 價 值 及 收 益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

2026.11.17 
吳昆潮 C00269@ibfs.com.tw 
7788.TT 松川精密 
 趨勢不變 評價調升

[財務資訊更新，調升評價]   
   公司簡介 松川精密成立於 1974 年，主要從事繼電器之研發、製造及銷售，繼電器屬於自動控 制開關的一種，係以較小電流(訊號)連動大電流(電源)的元件。 
投資評等/目標價 重點摘要 
強力買進 348 
【前次投資建議】買進，348 
【大盤指數/股價】41040 / 178.5

➢ 高價庫存原料侵蝕毛利，3Q26 EPS 2.83 元  
松川精密 3Q26 營收 27.35 億元，QoQ+21.04%，YoY+78.84%，毛利
率 27.97%，稅後淨利 2.26 億元，QoQ-14.23%，YoY+103.23%，EPS 
2.83 元，低於投研部預期，主要歸因於毛利率季減 2.98 個百分點，包含
兩大因素：1)產品組合變化：完整認列台興營收，單季營收佔比 3.6%，
且高毛利儲能、AI 繼電器相關產品佔比下滑 1 至 2 個百分點；2)高價原
料成本影響：上半年白銀曾衝上每盎司 121.6 美元的歷史天價，公司 8~9
月出貨產品採用高價白銀原料。 
➢ 最壞情況已過，4Q26 獲利重返單季新高 
10 月營收 8.49 億元，MoM-3.19%，YoY+53.62%，達成率 32%，符
合預期，部分產品出貨受船期影響，致營收遞延認列，整體單季營收與
原先預估差異不大。毛利率方面，下半年白銀每季平均跌幅 12%~14%，
目前回落至 50 美元以下，原料平均成本已下降，10 月高價原料衝擊影
響性降低，毛利率回升，預期單季最壞情況已過，4Q26 毛利率回升至
29.11%，EPS 3.33 元，獲利重返成長。 
➢ AI 伺服器高壓直流架構，繼電器/接觸器產值增加近兩倍 
AI 伺服器從 54V 升級至±400V 或 800V 高壓直流架構，必須額外配置
高壓直流接觸器，技術門檻遠高於傳統產品，單價達到交流繼電器的 20
倍至 30 倍，投研部推測每機櫃至少需配置 12 至 28 顆不等，單機櫃產
值成長 198%。松川 225A/900V 產品已率先邁入小量出貨階段，直流接
觸器將是松川 2027 年的核心成長引擎，預估松川 2027 年 AI 伺服器繼
電器/接觸器營收成長 50%~60%，營收佔比 20.2%。 
➢ 評等調升至強力買進，目標價 348 元 
投研部預估松川精密 2026 年/2027 年 EPS 分別為 11.68 元及 15.58
元，2027 年各應用面展望樂觀，儲能及 AI 伺服器仍是主軸，雖然毛利
率較原先下修 1 個百點，但上修直流接觸器產值預估，加上費用控管優
於預期，2027 年整體獲利小幅上修。評價方面，目前松川本益比僅 11
倍，處於歷史 PE 區間 10X~30X 下緣，投資價值浮現，投研部將松川投
資評等，由買進調升至強力買進，目標價 348 元(2027F PE23X)。

松川精密 3Q26 營收 27.35 億元，QoQ+21.04%，YoY+78.84%，毛利率 27.97%，

### Page 3

- 3 -

個股報告 
民主黨勝選有利綠能產業發展，儲能剛性需求強 
根據產業統計，2026 年前三季全球儲能總併網量估計達 220 至 240 GWh，系統出貨
量約 420 GWh。受惠於第四季併網旺季，BloombergNEF 於 10 月將 2026 全年新增
裝機預估上調至 188 GW / 535 GWh，並預期 2027 年將進一步成長至 209 GW / 638 
GWh，容量年增 19.3%。太陽能方面，全球新增裝機在 2025 年創高後，2026 年受
中國電網消納限制影響，預計微幅回落至 584 至 638 GW；但海外市場逆勢成長 15%
至 313 GW。展望 2027 年，全球裝機預估將回升至 618 GW，產業呈現「光弱儲強」
趨勢，加速光儲一體化建置。 
綜合上述，全球光儲兩大核心市場皆集中於中美兩國，加上美國民主黨於 2026 年期
中選舉勝出並奪下國會主導權，綠能與清潔能源政策延續性獲得有力支撐，可望進一
步推升當地光儲建置動能。中美兩地領導廠商均為松川精密的核心客戶，受惠於美系
客戶新一代儲能櫃容量由 3.9 MWh 提升至 5 MWh，工作電流增加帶動接觸器規格與
單價雙雙看漲，預估 2027 年松川在儲能、太陽能及微電網業務將維持 10%~15%的
成長。 
AI 伺服器高壓直流架構，繼電器/接觸器產值增加近兩倍 
AI 伺服器從 54V 升級至±400V 或 800V 高壓直流架構，機櫃配電單元(PDU)面臨嚴
峻的高壓電弧安全挑戰，必須額外配置高壓直流接觸器(DC Contactor)，為在毫秒內
強制切斷(滅弧)800V 高壓直流電弧，產品內部高度仰賴磁吹滅弧設計或真空密封充氣
(氫氣／氮氣)工藝，技術門檻遠高於傳統交流繼電器(AC Relay)，單價達到交流繼電器
的 20 倍至 30 倍。在用量方面，每座機櫃 PDU 至少需配置 12 顆，主流高階規格更
需 24 顆至 28 顆，單機櫃直流接觸器產值為既有交流繼電器的 2 至 3 倍。松川
225A/900V 產品已率先邁入小量出貨階段，未來隨著高壓直流架構全面放量，直流接
觸器將正式擔綱松川 2027 年的核心成長引擎。在額外增加直流接觸器需求下，投研
部推測高壓直流架構(不含自動轉換開關 ATS)，AI 伺服器繼電器/接觸器單機櫃產值將
從 7,000 元增加至 20,900 元，YoY+198%，預估松川 2027 年 AI 伺服器繼電器/接觸
器營收成長 50%~60%，營收佔比 20.2%。 
預估松川 2027 年 EPS 15.58 元，評等調升至強力買進，目標價 348 元 
預估松川精密 2026 年營收 95.52 億元，YoY+48.49%，毛利率 28.80%，稅後淨利
9.31 億元，YoY+246.03%，EPS 11.68 元；預估 2027 年 113.14 億元，YoY+18.45%，
毛利率 30.53%，稅後淨利 12.43 億元，YoY+33.42%，EPS 15.58 元。2027 年各應
用面展望樂觀，儲能及 AI 伺服器仍是主軸，雖然毛利率較原先下修 1 個百點，但上修
直流接觸器產值預估，加上費用控管優於預期，2027 年整體獲利小幅上修。評價方
面，目前松川本益比僅 11 倍，處於歷史 PE 區間 10X~30X 下緣，投資價值浮現，投
研部將松川投資評等，由買進調升至強力買進，目標價 348 元(2027F PE23X)。

## Document 274: A primer on fiscal measures debated in French elections_ Most candidates commit to restoring fiscal sustainability, with uneven credibility (2026-11-23)

Source PDF: all report pdf/A primer on fiscal measures debated in French elections_ Most candidates commit to restoring fiscal sustainability, with uneven credibility (2026-11-23).pdf
Report source: BNP Paribas
Date: 2026-11-23
Pages: 2
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

The bank
for a changing
world
7
economic-research.bnpparibas.comEcoWeek 26.43 / 23 November 2026FOCUS — FRENCH ELECTIONS
A PRIMER ON FISCAL MEASURES DEBATED IN FRENCH ELECTIONS: MOST CANDIDATES COMMIT TO RESTORING FISCAL
SUSTAINABILITY, WITH UNEVEN CREDIBILITY
1 Other growth-critical policy proposals will be reviewed in thematic deep-dives to be published over the coming months.
This note offers an overview of the measures proposed by presidential election candidates from the main political groups,

namely Marine Le Pen, Jean-Luc Mélenchon, Édouard Philippe, Raphaël Glucksmann, David Lisnard and Bruno Retailleau.
The focus here is on their proposals for public finances and pensions 1. Overall, budget plans go in the right direction but
rely on overly optimistic costs and revenue assumptions. However, in several cases a proposed “golden rule” enhances
credibility. The proposals regarding pensions improve on the status quo in most cases, including, in some cases, by intro-

ducing a capitalisation pillar. However, most safeguard costly features of the current system, without enough measures to
offset their impact fully.
KEY TAKEAWAYS
- The bulk of the candidates target a below 3% of GDP fiscal deficit by 2032. The budget proposals are often optimistic (or lack sufficient detail), which is a
common feature of previous presidential campaigns (e.g., in 2017, E. Macron proposed reducing public spending to 50% of GDP by 2022).

- A majority of candidates propose a “golden rule”, including the National Rally (Rassemblement national). A rule would lend credibility to their fiscal path (if
the planned measures are insufficient, the golden rule would require them to adopt additional measures, as it was done e.g. in Germany under the debt brake).
- The budget announcements made by several candidates — including the National Rally (RN) and La France Insoumise (LFI) — are expected to worsen the

fiscal deficit in 2027. According to our calculations, a one-year delay in fiscal consolidation would result in a 2 pp increase in the public debt-to-GDP ratio
by the end of that consolidation (in 2032, debt would reach 126.5% of GDP instead of 124.5% without this delay), and even more if the deficit or interest rate
conditions were to deteriorate significantly.

pensions), under a credible growth

Proposals on Public Finances: Optimistic assumptions in general, but a “golden rule,” proposed by a majority of candidates,
could boost the credibility of the fiscal path.

### Page 2

8
economic-research.bnpparibas.comEcoWeek 26.43 / 23 November 2026
The bank
for a changing
world
- In the absence of a golden rule, LFI’s commitment to fiscal consolidation remains uncertain. The candidate’s promises are very costly, leading to a clear in-
crease in the fiscal deficit. Some of the proposed measures are confiscatory in nature and would likely shrink the tax base. The candidate proposes no binding
safeguards to ensure that the deficit does not spiral out of control. Furthermore, his proposal to write off part of the public debt held by the ECB could be

interpreted as a way to avoid having to undertake fiscal consolidation efforts to stabilise public debt (not to mention its feasibility).
KEY TAKEAWAYS
- The candidates’ positions on pensions reflect real, underlying progress, particularly in order to mitigate the impact of the repeal of the Borne reform (retire-
ment age back to 62 years old) through savings on other parameters (contribution period, or tightened conditions such as an increased discount per missing

quarter).
- The prospect of fully indexing pensions, as proposed in several candidates' platforms, would be very costly (nearly 2% of GDP).
- Both measures (lowering the retirement age and full indexation) undermine the system’s long-term financial stability. Everything else equal, they also
reduce the size of the active labour force, a headwind to growth and fiscal sustainability.
- Édouard Philippe, David Lisnard and Bruno Retailleau advocate raising the retirement age to 65, and Édouard Philippe also calls for extending the contribu-

tion period to 45 years (while maintaining more favourable treatment for long careers and physically demanding jobs). The savings associated with extending
working careers represent a significant contribution to the announced fiscal consolidation effort.
- With regard to capitalisation, there are marked differences between the candidates who currently rule it out (J.L. Mélenchon, R. Glucksmann) and the others,
who have all made proposals along these lines (including the RN).

## Document 290: AI時代のサイバーレジリエンス

Report source: DIR
Date: 2026-11-25
Pages: 10
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

日本

株式会社大和総研 丸の内オフィス 〒100-6756 東京都千代田区丸の内一丁目 9 番 1 号 グラントウキョウ ノースタワー

2026 年 11 月 25 日 全 10 頁

AI 時代のサイバーレジリエンス 
攻撃の「速度・質・量」の変化に、企業はどう備えるか

AI アナリティックリサーチ室  主任研究員 田邉 美穂 
デジタルソリューション研究開発部 シニアセキュリティ・スペシャリスト 山崎 禎章

[要約] 
 国内でランサムウェア被害や不正アクセスによる情報漏洩が相次いでいる。こうした
現状は、AI によるソフトウェアの脆弱性発見能力の向上とも無関係ではないと考えら
れる。この能力は開発元による問題の早期発見や修正に役立つ一方、攻撃者にも利用
され得る。サイバー攻撃による被害は情報漏洩にとどまらず、業務の停止・遅延、長
期の復旧作業、多額の費用負担など、被害企業の事業継続だけではなく、取引先を含
むサプライチェーン全体に影響する経営上のリスクとなっている。 
 政府もサイバー攻撃を社会・経済活動の継続に関わるリスクと捉え、対応を強化して
いる。2026 年 10 月には、官民連携の強化や、攻撃に使われるサーバー等を警察・自衛
隊が無害化する措置などを柱とするサイバー対処能力強化法等の一部が施行された。
さらに 11 月には、情報収集能力の強化に向けて同法の改正を検討するとともに、既知
の脆弱性の修正などの基本対策、官民の情報共有、被害組織への支援などを盛り込ん
だサイバーセキュリティ緊急対策パッケージを取りまとめた。 
 AI は、攻撃対象の情報収集、フィッシングメールや攻撃プログラムの作成、攻撃の実
行など、サイバー攻撃のさまざまな場面で利用されていると考えられる。さらに、AI
エージェントを悪用し、脆弱性の探索から侵入の試行までを自律的に進める攻撃も可
能になりつつあり、攻撃の速度・質・量を変化させる可能性がある。他方、防御側で
も、攻撃を受ける前の脆弱性診断や、攻撃を受けた際の監視・分析、サイバー攻撃へ
の対応を支援する手段として AI の活用が期待されている。 
 AI の進歩により、企業にとってサイバーセキュリティの重要性は一段と高まるだろう。
企業は、AI が従来の基本対策を代替するものではないことを踏まえ、基本対策を着実
に進めるとともに、システムの提供元や委託先を含め、攻撃を受けた際にも事業を継
続・早期復旧できる体制を整える必要がある。その上で、自社の課題に応じて AI や外
部サービスも適切に活用し、サイバーレジリエンスを高めることが求められる。

### Page 2

1

2 / 10

1. 相次ぐサイバー攻撃による被害とその影響1 
増加するサイバー攻撃による被害と AI の影響 
国内で、ランサムウェア被害2や不正アクセスによる情報漏洩が相次いでいる。警察庁による
と、2026 年上半期に国内で報告されたランサムウェア被害は 123 件と、2020 年下半期の集計開
始以降、半期として過去最多となった3。また直近では、一般向けサービスや業務用システムへ
の不正アクセスによって、大量の個人情報が漏洩する事案も目立つ。2026 年 9 月以降に日本経
済新聞 電子版で新たに報道された日本国内の個人情報流出件数を、組織・案件ごとに集計した
結果、9 月の 9 件に対し、10 月は 204 件と急増した（図表 1）。 さらに複数の組織にまたがる同
一事案を 1 件にまとめた「共通事象」で数え直しても、9 月の 7 件に対し、10 月は 108 件と 15
倍を超える。 
図表 1 2026 年 9 月以降に報道された日本国内の個人情報流出件数の推移（日次、単位：件）

（注）期間は 2026 年 9 月 1 日～10 月 31 日。日本経済新聞 電子版「個人情報流出」トピックで報じられた国
内の企業、公的機関、団体が対象。組織・案件は組織と案件の組み合わせで、流出の疑いを含む。共通事象
は、共通して使うサービスや委託先への攻撃など、複数の組織にまたがる案件を 1 件として数えた値。7 日移
動平均は当日を含む後方 7 日間の平均。日付は記事の公開日（同一案件は最初の報道日）で、攻撃の発生日
ではない。集計方法の詳細は、新田尭之「個人情報流出はどの程度増えたのか？」（大和総研コラム、2026 年
10 月30 日）を参照。 
（出所）日本経済新聞 電子版より大和総研作成

こうした現状には、AI によるソフトウェアの脆弱性発見能力の向上も無関係ではないと考え
られる。AI による脆弱性発見能力の向上は、開発元が未知の問題を早期に把握し、修正する上
で有用である。一方、攻撃者が同じ能力を使えば、修正される前の脆弱性を見つけて攻撃に悪
用できる。攻撃者が脆弱性を発見し、攻撃に悪用するまでの時間が短くなれば、企業側の対応
が間に合わない事態が生じやすくなる。 
2026 年 4 月に米 Anthropic が、サイバーセキュリティ分野で高い能力を持つ汎用言語モデル
「Claude Mythos Preview 」を発表した。その後も、米国や中国などの複数の企業から、高度な

（注）主要なソフトウェア・ハードウェア企業およびオープンソース団体 21 組織が、公表された脆弱性情報
を識別・整理し、共有する国際的な取り組みである CVE Program を通じて公表した脆弱性情報（CVE レコー
ド）のうち、CVSS（脆弱性の深刻度を評価する指標）に基づく深刻度が、「High」（ 7.0 ～8.9 ）または

脆弱性発見能力をはじめとする AI の性能向上がサイバー攻撃にもたらす変化については、第
2 章で詳しく整理する。

サイバー攻撃による企業活動への影響も深刻化 
サイバー攻撃では、個人情報や機密情報の漏洩に目が向きやすい。しかし、影響はそれだけ
にとどまらない。ランサムウェアなどによりシステムやデータが利用できなくなれば、受発注、
製造、販売、物流、顧客対応など、幅広い業務が停止・遅延する可能性がある。サイバー攻撃
の影響は、情報管理上の問題だけでなく、企業が重要な業務を継続できるかという経営上の問
題にも及ぶ。 
被害からの復旧には、長い時間を要する場合もある。警察庁が 2026 年上半期のランサムウェ
ア被害を調査したところ、復旧期間について有効回答が得られた 48 件のうち、18 件（37.5％）
は 1 週間以上を要し、13 件（27.1％）は調査時点でも復旧中だった（図表 3 左）。また、日本
情報経済社会推進協会（JIPDEC）が 2026 年 1 月に実施した「企業 IT 利活用動向調査 2026」で
は、ランサムウェアの感染被害を経験した企業のうち、システムやデータの復旧に成功した割
合は半数を下回る。さらに、復旧に成功した企業では約 7 割が 1 カ月以内に復旧している一方、
復旧できなかった企業では約 4 割が断念するまでに 1 カ月超を費やしている。この結果は、長

### Page 4

4 / 10

期間復旧作業を続けても、復旧に至らない場合があることを示している4。これら二つの調査は、
調査対象や回答方法が異なるため単純な比較はできないが、復旧が長期に及び得る点は共通し
ている。 
復旧には相応の費用もかかる。前述の警察庁の調査では、調査・復旧に要した費用について
有効回答が得られた 35 件のうち、21 件（60.0％）が総額 1,000 万円以上を要したと回答して
いる。このうち、1 億円以上の回答も 4 件あり、全有効回答の 11.4％を占めた（図表 3 右）。こ
れらは被害に伴う調査・復旧費用であり、業務停止による売上機会の喪失や顧客・取引先への
対応、信用の低下などを含めれば、企業が被る損失は調査で把握された金額を上回る可能性が
ある。 
さらに、影響は被害を受けた企業の中だけに収まるとは限らない。業務を支える子会社や委
託先、取引先、クラウドサービスなどが攻撃を受ければ、取引関係を通じて複数の企業へ波及
する可能性がある。KPMG ジャパンが日本経済新聞社と共同で行った調査では、サイバー攻撃に
よる業務上の被害が生じた経路として国内の委託先・取引先が最も多かった。また、国内の委
託先・取引先を経由した攻撃の有無を把握できていない企業は３割弱に上り、委託先のセキュ
リティ対策を継続的に把握する仕組みに課題があることが示されている5。サイバー攻撃は、被
害企業の情報システム上の問題にとどまらず、取引先を含むサプライチェーン全体に影響を及
ぼし得る経営上のリスクとして捉える必要がある。 
図表 3 2026 年上半期のランサムウェア被害からの復旧期間・費用

（出所）警察庁サイバー警察局「令和 8 年上半期におけるサイバー空間をめぐる脅威の情勢等について」（令和
8 年9 月）、p.11 より大和総研作成

政府もサイバー攻撃への対応を強化 
こうした中、政府もサイバー攻撃への対応を強化している。2026 年 10 月 1 日には、能動的
サイバー防御（サイバー攻撃の兆候を把握し、重大な被害の未然防止や拡大防止を図る取り組
み）の実現に向けた「サイバー対処能力強化法」および「同整備法」（サイバー対処能力強化

法等）の一部が施行された6。同法等による取り組みは、①官民連携の強化、②通信情報の利用、
③アクセス・無害化措置の三つを柱とし、このうち今回施行されたのは①と③に関する規定で
ある。②の通信情報の利用は 2027 年秋以降に開始される予定だ。 
①官民連携では、電力、鉄道、通信、金融などの基幹インフラ事業者に対し、重要なシステ
ムに関係する機器の製品名などの資産情報の届出や、不正アクセスや悪意のあるプログラムの
実行など、一定のサイバーインシデントの報告を求める。その上で政府は、基幹インフラ事業
者から届け出られた資産情報と、未公表のものを含む脆弱性情報を突き合わせ、該当する事業
者に速やかに注意喚起を行う。また、政府と民間で脅威情報を共有するための協議会も設置さ
れた。 
③アクセス・無害化措置では、サイバー攻撃によって重大な危害が発生するおそれがある場
合など一定の要件の下で、警察や自衛隊が攻撃に使用されているサーバーなどにアクセスし、
不正なプログラムの停止・削除などを行うことが可能になった。 
こうした制度の運用が始まる一方、政府は、日本を標的としたサイバー攻撃の兆候や被害を
より迅速に把握するため、情報収集能力をさらに強化する必要があるとして、同法の改正を検
討する方針だ7。 
さらに、政府は 2026 年 11 月 6 日、相次ぐサイバー攻撃や情報漏洩を受け、「サイバーセキュ
リティ緊急対策パッケージ」を取りまとめた8。政府は同パッケージで、昨今の情報漏洩事案に
は、既知の脆弱性の放置や設定の不備など、自組織や委託先の基本的な対策が不十分だった例
が多いと指摘した。さらに、AI の進歩によって、こうした隙が短時間かつ大規模に突かれるよ
うになったとの認識も示している。その上で、①「AI も活用した基本的な対策の徹底」、②
「情報収集・集約、捜査・分析体制の強化」、③「サイバー攻撃への対処の戦略的実施」、④
「被害者支援・被害拡大防止策の強化」を柱に掲げた。AI を活用した脆弱性診断や、セキュリ
ティベンダー、クラウド事業者、先端 AI 企業などとの情報共有、官民の専門人材による被害組
織への支援などを通じ、社会全体でサイバー攻撃の被害を抑え、早期の対応・復旧につなげる
方針である。 
サイバー攻撃を社会・経済活動の継続に関わるリスクとして捉え、社会全体で備える動きも
国内外で進んでいる。政府は、サイバー攻撃などによる大規模な停電や通信障害、断水などが
長期化し、国民生活に深刻な影響を及ぼす場合には、災害対策基本法上の「災害」として対応
する方針を示した9。海外でも、英国政府が 2026 年 10 月、自然災害からサイバー攻撃まで幅広

ンを開始したことが話題となった10。サイバー攻撃を、自然災害などと同様に社会で備えるべ

2. AI がサイバー攻撃をどう変えるのか 
サイバー攻撃における AI 利用の現状 
昨今のサイバー攻撃による被害の増加を踏まえると、サイバー攻撃における AI の利用は進ん
でいると考えて間違いないだろう。AI は、攻撃対象に関する情報収集、フィッシングメールの
作成、攻撃プログラムの作成、脆弱性の探索、横展開、攻撃の実行など、攻撃の準備から実行
に至るまでのさまざまな場面で利用されていると考えられる。攻撃者は AI を利用することで、
言語の壁などから従来は狙いにくかった対象も攻撃できるようになった。さらに、従来よりも
短時間で準備を進め、より多くの対象に攻撃を仕掛けることが可能になっている。 
最近、大きく次元が変わったと考えられるのが、AI エージェントがサイバー攻撃に利用され
る事例である。LLM（大規模言語モデル）を用いて脆弱性診断・ペネトレーションテスト11を自
動化する、ARTEX や Strix といった AI エージェント型ツールが登場している。これらのツール
が攻撃者に悪用され、実際のサイバー攻撃に用いられたという報道がある1213。これらのツール
は脆弱性診断・ペネトレーションテストを行うために攻撃的なスキルが組み込まれており、対
象となるシステムの情報やどのようなテストを行うかといった情報を与えると、脆弱性の探索、
侵入方法の検討、試行などを自律的に進める。本来の目的に反して、許可を得た診断対象では
なく無許可の攻撃対象のシステム情報を与えると、そのままツールが攻撃を実行するという流
れだ。 
ただし、実際のサイバー攻撃について、攻撃者が AI を使ったかどうかを被害者の立場から証
明することは基本的には難しい。攻撃者が利用していたツールの管理画面が誤って公開されて
いる場合を除けば、攻撃のパターンから AI が使われたかどうかを推測するしかできない。ま
た、AI を使った攻撃であっても、AI 特有の対処方法があるわけではないため、攻撃に AI が使
われているかどうかは本質的な問題ではないともいえる点は注意が必要である。

10 GOV.UK Campaigns“Be Ready”, last accessed November 24, 2026; Cabinet Office and The Rt Hon Dan 
Jarvis MBE MP “Households encouraged to take small, simple steps to stay prepared in an 
emergency”, October 22, 2026. 
11 ペネトレーションテスト（侵入テスト）とは、実際の攻撃者に近い手法でシステムへの侵入を試み、セキュ
リティ上の弱点や侵入の可能性を検証するテストを指す。

October 7, 2026.

2026.

AI が変える攻撃の「速度・質・量」 
AI がサイバー攻撃に与える影響は、「速度・質・量」の三つの観点で整理できる（図表 4）。 
第一は、攻撃速度の増加である。ソフトウェアの脆弱性が公表されると、攻撃者は公開情報
や脆弱性を修正する更新プログラム（パッチ）の内容を分析し、その脆弱性を悪用するプログ
ラムを生成することができる。AI が攻撃者に悪用されると、脆弱性公表から攻撃への転用まで
の時間が短縮されることが考えられる。その結果、企業側が脆弱性を修正するまでに与えられ
る時間も短くなってしまう。 
第二は、攻撃者の能力の底上げ、いわば攻撃の質の変化である。従来、高度な攻撃には、プ
ログラミング、ネットワーク、OS などさまざまな領域について一定の専門知識が必要だった。
AI が調査やコード生成を支援することで、経験の少ない攻撃者でも、従来と同等レベルのサイ
バー攻撃を行うことが可能になった。 
第三は、攻撃量の増加である。AI エージェントを悪用したサイバー攻撃では、複数のエージ
ェントを同時並行で稼働させることで、多数の企業やシステムに対して、一斉に攻撃を行うこ
とが可能になった。従来は企業ごとにシステムの特性が異なることが一斉攻撃の障壁となって
いたが、AI によって対象に合わせて攻撃を調整して仕掛けることが容易となり、この障壁は低
下している。 
もっとも、AI によって攻撃者側が完全に有利になるとも限らない点は触れておきたい。経験
の少ない攻撃者が AI を利用した場合に、多くの痕跡を残すことがあり、身元を隠すための対策
が不十分であることもある。多数の痕跡から従来よりも攻撃者を追跡することが容易となる可
能性もある。

3. 防御側の考え方と AI の活用 
企業に求められる基本的な考え方 
サイバー攻撃への対応では、攻撃を完全に防ぐことだけを目指すのではなく、攻撃を受ける
ことも前提に、被害を抑えながら重要な事業やサービスを継続し、早期に復旧できるよう備え
る「サイバーレジリエンス」の考え方が重視されてきた14。具体的には、脆弱性への対応や認
証の強化などによって侵入されにくくするとともに、侵入された場合にも被害が広がらないよ
う検知・対応し、バックアップなどを活用して早期に復旧できる体制を整えることが基本とな
る。 
こうした基本的な考え方は、AI の登場によって大きく変わるものではない。一方、攻撃側で
AI の活用が進み、攻撃の速度や質、量が変化すれば、防御側にもそれに応じた対応が求められ
る。

防御への AI 利用とは 
攻撃側での AI の活用が進む一方で、防御側でも AI を利用する動きが広がっている。セキュ
リティ領域での AI の利用は「攻撃を受ける前の対策」と「攻撃を受けた際の対応支援」の二つ
に分けられる（図表5）。 
一つ目は、AI を利用した脆弱性診断やペネトレーションテストである。AI を利用した脆弱性
診断では、AI にソースコードやシステムの情報を与えて分析させることによって、脆弱性の候
補を網羅的に抽出する。AI を利用したペネトレーションテストは、AI に実際の攻撃者に近い形
でシステムへの侵入を行わせて、そのシステムで特に有効な攻撃手法を調べるという方法であ
る。攻撃者側が頻繁に脆弱性探索や侵入を試みている現状からすると、防御側も先回りしてこ
れらを定期的に行い、事前に対策していくことが必要になっていくと考えられる。 
二つ目は、セキュリティ監視やインシデント対応での利用である。企業が管理する機器・シ
ステムからは日々大量のログやセキュリティ警告が発生する。従来の SOC（セキュリティ・オ
ペレーション・センター）による、サイバー攻撃の兆候の監視・分析や対応に加えて、AI にも
これらの分析を行わせるといった形である。例えば、同じ利用者による通常とは異なる場所か
らのログインなど、人間の SOC 担当者では気付きにくい攻撃パターンなども発見することが期
待できる。また、過去事例や外部からの脅威情報を照合して、自社が攻撃されていないかを発
見する際にも AI と対話しながら評価を行うといった利用方法が考えられる。これらは AI を用
いた SOC である「AI-SOC」や脅威の兆候を能動的に探索する「脅威ハンティング」と呼ばれる
活用方法である。

14 サイバーセキュリティ戦略本部「サイバーセキュリティ 2026（2025 年度年次報告・2026 年度年次計画）」
（令和 8 年 7 月 31 日）においても、「幅広い主体による社会全体のサイバーセキュリティ及びレジリエンス
の向上」が政策の柱の一つに掲げられている。

### Page 9

1

9 / 10

ただし、AI が脆弱性や異常を検知したとしても、その妥当性を検証しなければならない点に
は注意が必要である。特に、セキュリティ監視において AI の判断が誤っていた場合、正常な業
務を停止させることにもつながるため、慎重な評価が求められる。 
AI は、資産管理、脆弱性対応、認証強化、バックアップといった従来の基本対策を代替する
ものではない、という点にも触れておきたい。AI によるセキュリティ強化は従来の対策の上に
成り立つものであり、これらが不十分な状態では、AI が問題を発見できたとしても早期の対応
を行うことは難しい。そのため、まずは従来の対策が十分にできていない領域を優先して対応
し、その上で AI の導入を検討すべきである。

4. 企業は基本対策を土台に対応力の強化を 
AI によってサイバー攻撃が高度化・高速化・大規模化する可能性がある中、企業には、防御、
検知、対応、復旧に要する時間を短縮することが求められる。前章でみたように、防御に AI を
活用することで、セキュリティ担当者による判断や対応を迅速化できる可能性があるものの、
AI 自体は従来の基本対策を代替するものではない。まずは、守るべきシステムやデータと対
応・復旧の優先順位を明確にし、基本対策を着実に進める必要がある。 
特に、専門人材や予算が限られる中小企業では、高度な監視・対応体制を自社だけで構築す
るのは難しい。独立行政法人情報処理推進機構（IPA）は、中小企業がはじめに取り組むべき
基本的な対策として、①OS やソフトウェアを常に最新の状態に保つ、②ウイルス対策ソフトを
導入する、③パスワードを強化する、④共有設定を見直す、⑤バックアップを取る、⑥脅威や
攻撃を受ける前の対策 攻撃を受けた際の対応支援

### Page 10

1

10 / 10

攻撃の手口を知る、という「情報セキュリティ 6 か条」を示している15。また、政府は、サイ
バーセキュリティ緊急対策パッケージにおいて、中小企業向けの「サイバーセキュリティお助
け隊サービス16」として、復旧やバックアップなどの支援を行う方針を示している。中小企業
は、まずは実施可能な基本対策から着実に取り組むとともに、利用可能な外部サービスや今後
具体化される支援策も念頭に置きつつ、自社の対応体制を段階的に整えていくことが重要だろ
う。 
自社内の対策に加え、システムの提供元や委託先を含めたリスク管理も欠かせない。業務シ
ステムやクラウドサービスの提供元、システムの運用委託先などでサイバー攻撃や情報漏洩、
システム障害が発生した場合も、自社の事業に影響を及ぼしかねない。サービスの選定時だけ
でなく、利用開始後も提供元や委託先のセキュリティ対策を継続的に把握し、問題発生時の報
告や対応、復旧に関する役割分担を確認しておく必要がある。委託元が委託先に求める対策水
準を共通基準で示し、その実施状況を確認する政府の「サプライチェーン強化に向けたセキュ
リティ対策評価制度（SCS 評価制度）」が、2026 年度末頃から開始される予定である17。提供元
や委託先が評価を取得している場合、その評価結果も対策状況を把握する材料の一つとなろう。 
AI の進歩によってサイバー攻撃が高度化・高速化・大規模化する中、企業にとってサイバー
セキュリティの重要性は一層高まるだろう。こうした変化は、自社の対策や対応体制を見直す
機会にもなり得る。攻撃に AI が使われているかどうかにかかわらず、企業に求められる基本的
な備えが大きく変わるわけではない。基本対策を着実に進めた上で、AI の特性や限界を理解し、
自社の課題に応じて適切に活用することで、攻撃を受けても事業を継続し、早期に復旧できる
サイバーレジリエンスを高めていくことが求められる。 
以上

### Page 1

Anthropic ⾯临多重压⼒：⾼管上市后减持、 DeepSeek 等开源模型加剧竞争，数据中⼼建
设放缓及美国利率上⾏也可能拖累其业务扩张和未来估值。不过， MSCI 即将将其纳⼊全球

撑。
摘要：
上市不⾜⼀周， Anthropic 便跌破 IPO 发⾏价。这家备受市场追捧的前沿 AI 公司迅速遭遇股价回
撤，也让投资者重新审视 AI 企业的⾼估值能否得到持续⽀撑。
周⼆盘前， Anthropic 股价⼀度跌破 83 美元的 IPO 发⾏价，最新报 83.4 美元，跌幅约 5% 。相⽐ 11
⽉ 19 ⽇上市⾸⽇ 111.50 美元的开盘价，股价已明显回落。作为⾸家登陆公开市场的前沿 AI 公
司， Anthropic 的表现也被视为观察 AI ⾏业投资情绪的重要⻛向标。
值得注意的是，此次下跌发⽣在 AI 板块整体情绪有所回暖之际。英伟达周⼀宣布⻩仁勋复任⾸席
执⾏官，带动 AI 相关股票普遍上涨，但 Anthropic 未能跟随反弹。与此同时，标普 500 指数和道琼

斯⼯业平均指数上周先后进⼊调整区间，科技股整体仍⾯临抛售压⼒。
多重⻛险叠加， Anthropic 股价承压
Anthropic 股价跌破发⾏价后，投资者开始重新审视公司⾯临的多重压⼒。
⾸先，内部⼈⼠减持增加了市场对股份供给的担忧。根据周⼀晚间提交的监管⽂件， Anthropic
⾸席执⾏官 Dario Amodei 与总裁 Daniela Amodei 在上市次⽇分别向承销商出售逾 337 万股股份，
套现⾦额均超过 2.8 亿美元。与此同时，承销商⾏使超额配售权，将 IPO 总募资规模推升⾄ 935 亿
美元的创纪录⽔平。
其次，开源模型的快速发展正在挑战 Anthropic 的商业模式。DeepSeek 、 Mistral 等开发商持续
推动开源权重模型普及，⽤⼾可以⾃⾏下载、部署和定制模型。 Anthropic 此前已在招股⽂件中
警⽰，开源模型竞争可能对其产品定价形成压⼒。
宏观环境也可能影响公司的增⻓预期。 StoneX ⾼级市场分析师 Fiona Cincotta 指出，如果数据中

⼼建设放缓， Anthropic 的模型开发进度可能受到影响，进⽽拖累未来营收和估值。此外，利率

历史上明星 IPO 也曾破发，被动资⾦或提供⽀撑
从历史来看，明星科技公司 IPO 上市初期跌破发⾏价并不罕⻅。今年 6 ⽉创下全球最⼤ IPO 纪录

## Document 303: Anthropic让AI重构自家核心系统：CPU消耗骤减70%，内存占用狂降80%，响应速度飙升！

Source PDF: all report pdf/Anthropic让AI重构自家核心系统：CPU消耗骤减70%，内存占用狂降80%，响应速度飙升！.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-11-26
Pages: 5
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

为应对流量暴增， Anthropic 利⽤⾃家 AI 将核⼼ API 从 Python 全⾯迁移⾄ Rust 。 Claude 编
写了超 80% 的代码，实现零停机替换，使 CPU 与内存消耗骤降 70% 和 80% ，响应提速
20% 。此次创举证明了 AI 主导⾼⻛险底层重构的安全可⾏，标志着软件⼯程正式迈⼊ “AI ⽣
成 + 系统验证 ” 的新范式。
摘要：
在科技圈，对⼀个庞⼤且正在⾼速运转的⽣产系统进⾏ “ 彻底重写 ” ，往往被⼯程师们视为技术上
的 “ ⾃杀⾏为 ” 。
但 11 ⽉ 25 ⽇，顶级 AI 公司 Anthropic 宣布完成了⼀项堪称奇迹的⼯程：他们让⾃家的 AI ⼤模型
Claude 亲⾃操⼑，将核⼼的 Messages API 从 Python 全⾯迁移到了 Rust 。
结果令⼈震惊：在没有⼀分钟停机、没有冻结任何新功能开发的情况下，新系统每请求的 CPU
消耗骤减约 70% ，内存占⽤狂降超过 80% ，中位数⾸ token 响应时间（ TTFT ）缩短了约 20% ！

Anthropic 究竟是如何利⽤ AI 完成这场 “ 给⻜⾏中的⻜机换引擎 ” 的壮举的？这场史诗级迁移⼜给未
来的软件⼯程带来了怎样的启⽰？
流量暴增 1800 倍， Python 不堪重负
回到 2023 年， Anthropic 的 Messages API 最初是⽤ Python 编写的。
当时的逻辑极其简单，按⼀位⼯程师的说法， “ 基本就是个请求转发器 ”—— 把⽤⼾请求递给模
型，再把模型⽣成的回复原样传回。
但到了 2026 年秋天， API 的流量⽐ 2024 年初⾜⾜暴增了约 1800 倍！
随着功能的复杂化，每⼀个请求都需要经过验证、构建提⽰词、分词和安全分类等繁琐步骤。
Python 在创建对象、释放内存以及任务交接时产⽣的系统开销，让原本的架构不堪重负。
⼯程师们最初尝试了 “ 渐进式重写 ”—— 只⽤ Rust 重写解析模型输出的部分。但结果适得其反：每
⼀个 token 必须在 Python 和 Rust 之间来回穿梭三次，反⽽增加了系统的复杂度和开销。
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⻅闻历 搜索

### Page 2

彻底转向 Rust 成了唯⼀的出路。⽽此时的 Claude 已经⾜够强⼤，到了 2026 年 5 ⽉， Anthropic 合
并的代码中，有超过 80% 是由 Claude 亲⾃编写的。
实际上，Claude 仅⽤了⼀周时间，就写出了 Rust 版本 API 核⼼请求路径的初稿。
但真正的问题来了：代码写出来容易，怎么证明它没⽑病？
“ ⽣成器 - 验证器鸿沟 ” ： AI 写代码容易，查错太难
在软件⼯程中，存在着著名的 “ ⽣成器 - 验证器鸿沟 ” （ Generator-verifier gap ）。
就像解数独，检查答案很容易，但找出答案很难。然⽽在实时的⽣产系统中，情况恰恰相反：
让 Claude ⽣成代码很容易，但验证代码是否 100% 正确却难如登天。
Anthropic ⾯临着⼏个极度棘⼿的挑战：
为了解决这个死结， Anthropic 决定引⼊“ 影⼦测试 ” 系统（ Shadowing ），直接把线上的 Python
⽣产系统当作判断对错的 “ ⻩⾦标准 ” 。
但⼤模型接⼝的影⼦测试，有两个传统软件没有的死⽳：

测试⽤例不可靠：原有的 700 多个集成测试与 Python ⾼度耦合。当 Claude 把测试也移植到
Rust 时，很多断⾔被⽆意间 “ 削弱 ” 了，导致错误代码也能蒙混过关。
测试不完整： API 有数⼗种功能配置，仅 20 个⼆元选项就能产⽣超百万种组合。靠测试根本
⽆法穷尽真实⽣产环境中的所有边缘情况。
AI 的⾮确定性： Claude 对同⼀个请求的回复每次都不⼀样。如果输出结果有差异，你很难判
断这到底是系统 Bug ，还是⼤模型正常的波动。
隐私限制：⼯程师绝对不能查看客⼾的真实请求和模型回复，这意味着他们⽆法通过 “ ⾁眼看
⽂本 ” 来找 Bug 。
⼤模型输出具有随机性：  同⼀个问题问 Claude 两次，每次措辞都可能不同。传统对⽐⼯具如
果直接⽐对最终回复，会把所有正常波动当成噪⾳过滤掉，反⽽漏掉真正的 Bug 。
严禁重复扣费和计次：  同⼀笔真实请求绝不能跑两次模型，否则会重复消耗⽤⼾的额度并产
⽣双倍账单。

### Page 3

为此， Anthropic 设计了⼀套精巧的 “ 单次推理、双端⽐对 ” 架构：在每次请求中，模型只运⾏⼀
次，系统将模型吐出的同⼀份 Token 流回放给 Rust 版本。在此基础上，严格⽐对模型调⽤前（输
⼊构建）和模型调⽤后（输出解析与⽤量统计）的每⼀个中间步骤，要求两者的处理结果做到
字节级完全⼀致。
同时，为了严守⽤⼾隐私红线，系统在⽐对时绝对不记录、不查看任何⽤⼾的请求原⽂或模型
回复内容。那么，⼯程师和 Claude 代理（ Agent ）如何在 “ 盲盒 ” 状态下精准定位 Bug ？
答案是提取差异的 “ 结构形状 ” ，依靠检查点（ Checkpointing ）和指纹（ Fingerprinting ）锁定病
灶：
通过这种⽅式，系统⽣成了⼀份份不包含任何⽤⼾隐私⽂本的 “ 差异记录 ” ，并将其⾃动派发给
Claude 智能体（ Agents ）去修复。
检查点（ Checkpointing ）：对⽐两个版本在处理请求时的每⼀个步骤（如验证、构建提⽰
词）。第⼀个出现差异的步骤，就是 Bug 的藏⾝之处。

指纹（ Fingerprint ）：记录差异发⽣的位置和⻓度。他们发现，同⼀个 Bug 往往会在不同的
请求上留下相同的 “ 指纹 ”

### Page 4

⻛险提⽰及免责条款
三重验证循环： AI 与⼈类的完美协同
⾯对海量的差异记录， Anthropic 设计了⼀套由 AI 主导、⼈类把关的 “ 三重验证循环 ” ：
软件⼯程的范式转移
Anthropic 将这次迁移⽐作著名的哲学悖论 “ 忒修斯之船 ” 。
传统的重构往往需要把船停在港⼝，⼀块块替换⽊板。⽽ Claude 的强⼤能⼒，让他们得以在旧
船全速航⾏的同时，在旁边建造了⼀艘功能完全相同的新船。智能体不断调整新船，直到它与
旧船的航⾏轨迹严丝合缝。当确认⽆误后，乘客被平滑地转移到了这艘更快、更⾼效的 Rust 新
船上。
这次史诗级的重构，为整个科技界留下了极其宝贵的经验：
Anthropic ⽤⾃家的核⼼系统证明了：在 AI 的加持下，⻛险极⾼的底层重构不仅可⾏，⽽且可以
⽆⽐优雅。未来的软件⼯程，正在翻开由 “AI ⽣成  + 系统验证 ” 主导的全新篇章！
快速内循环（ Inner Loop ）： AI 的沙盒游乐场  智能体在⼀个云端沙盒中运⾏。拿到 “ 指纹 ” 后，

AI 会⾃⼰猜测 Bug 原因，⾃⼰编写测试请求，并在不到⼀秒的时间内对⽐ Python 和 Rust 的运
⾏结果。如果猜错了就重试；如果复现了 Bug ， AI 就会修改 Rust 代码，直到两者完全匹配，
然后提交 PR （拉取请求）。
慢速外循环（ Outer Loop ）：真实流量的试⾦⽯  内循环的修复⽅案必须经过⼈类⼯程师的审
查。合并后，代码会被部署到影⼦⽣产环境中，接受真实流量的检验。如果 “ 指纹 ” 消失，说明
修复成功；如果还在，任务就会被打回内循环。
记忆循环（ Memory Loop ）：让 AI 不再犯同样的错  ⼈类⼯程师在审查中做出的纠正（例如拒
绝 AI 为了⾛捷径⽽写的临时代码），会被记录到 AI 的 “ 记忆 ” 中（如 CLAUDE.md ⽂件）。未来的
智能体在⼯作前会先阅读这些规则，甚⾄会⾃动全局搜索并修复其他地⽅犯下的同类错误。
验证是新的瓶颈：当 AI 写代码不再是难点时，团队的野⼼上限，取决于你能多快地 “ 验证 ”AI 的
结果。
⼈类退居 “ 边缘 ”：在重构初期，⼈类⼯程师还在与 AI ⼀起抓 Bug ；但随着系统成熟，⼈类逐

渐退到了验证循环的边缘，专注于制定规则、提供⾼杠杆的判断，让 Claude 实现⾃我驱动。

### Page 5

市场有⻛险，投资需谨慎。本⽂不构成个⼈投资建议，也未考虑到个别⽤⼾特殊的投资⽬标、财务状况或需要。⽤⼾应考虑本⽂
中的任何意⻅、观点或结论是否符合其特定状况。据此投资，责任⾃负。

## Document 363: First Impressions_ NZ retail trade, September quarter 2026 - New Zealanders fill up the car and head back to the mall (2026-11-26)

Source PDF: all report pdf/First Impressions_ NZ retail trade, September quarter 2026 - New Zealanders fill up the car and head back to the mall (2026-11-26).pdf
Report source: Westpac IQ
Date: 2026-11-26
Pages: 10
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

#retail #newzealand
First Impressions: NZ retail trade, September quarter 2026 - New
Zealanders fill up the car and head back to the mall
By Satish Ranchhod
Senior Economist, Westpac NZ
09:19 November 23 2026 Share
The September quarter saw a larger than expected rise in spending, including firmness in
core categories.
September quarter retail sales
Retail sales (volume of goods sold): +2.2% (Prev: -0.4%)

### Page 2

Westpac f/c: 1.4%, Market: +0.5% 
Core retail sales (volume of goods sold): 1.4% (Prev: 0.8%)

Nominal retail sales: 2.4% (Prev: 1.0%)

Year to September
Volume of goods sold: 3.5%
Nominal sales: 7.0%

New Zealanders fill up the car and head back to the mall.
The September quarter retail sales report showed a sharp recovery in spending. Nominal spending
was up 2.4% over the quarter, and the amount of goods that New Zealanders are actually taking home
was up a solid 2.2%.
Today’s result was above our top of market forecast for a 1.4% quarterly rise, and well above the
median market forecast for +0.5%.
While the strong September quarter result is welcome news, we are taking it with a grain of salt. Much
of the rise in spending was related to fuel spending, which was boosted by the fall in fuel prices over
the September quarter, and fuel prices have step higher since then.
However, even excluding fuel, we have seen firmness in spending appetites. Core spending (excl. fuel
and motor vehicles) was up 1.4% over the September quarter and is 4.7% higher than this time last
year. 
Looking at the breakdown of spending, we did see a rise in grocery purchases. Meanwhile, spending in
discretionary categories was mixed – there has been lower spending on items like furnishings and
hardware, while spending on hospitality, electronics and recreational items is up. Overall, were left with
a picture of gradually improving spending appetites.

Outlook
Today’s report reinforces our expectations that the economy is moving into stronger phase. We expect
that spending levels will continue to firm through the holiday season and 2027. However, that recovery
is still likely to be gradual due to the combination of ongoing high inflation, a soft labour market and
rising interest rates. We also expect the recovery will be uneven, with those regions with strong links to
the agricultural and tourism sectors outperforming metro regions, like Auckland and Wellington.

Implications for GDP growth
We’re forecasting GDP growth of 0.6% in the September quarter. Today’s result was above
expectations. We’ll take a closer look at how our forecast for GDP growth is shaping up over the next
couple of weeks as additional data on September quarter activity is released.

Satish Ranchhod, Senior Economist

## Document 429: SHEIN三季度盈利回升，欧洲市场寻求2027年下半年复苏

Source PDF: all report pdf/SHEIN三季度盈利回升，欧洲市场寻求2027年下半年复苏.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-11-26
Pages: 3
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

全球贸易政策的变化，正在推动 SHEIN （希⾳）重新调整区域经营策略。  11 ⽉ 25 ⽇，
SHEIN 公布 2...
摘要：
全球贸易政策的变化，正在推动 SHEIN （希⾳）重新调整区域经营策略。
11 ⽉ 25 ⽇， SHEIN 公布 2026 年第三季度业绩，实现净收⼊ 102.71 亿美元，同⽐下降 5.9% ；经营
利润 7.43 亿美元，同⽐增⻓ 17.4% 。
期内净利润同⽐增⻓  654.5% ⾄  46.48 亿美元，其中  40.75 亿美元来⾃上市相关的优先股公允
价值变动等⾮现⾦⼀次性收益；经调整净利润为 7.9 亿美元，同⽐增⻓ 26.8% 、环⽐增⻓
246.5% ，⾼于⾼盛此前预测的 6.74 亿美元。
三季度是欧盟取消低价值商品关税豁免后的⾸个完整季度。 SHEIN 欧洲市场收⼊降幅进⼀步扩
⼤，但美国市场需求已出现企稳迹象，拉美等新兴市场则继续贡献增量。
今年 7 ⽉ 1 ⽇起，欧盟取消 150 欧元以下包裹的关税豁免，并对每类商品征收 3 欧元关税。

为覆盖新增成本， SHEIN ⾃⼆季度起上调欧洲市场的商品售价，并根据投资回报率重新分配营
销资源。
相关举措的影响在三季度进⼀步显现。期内 SHEIN 欧洲市场收⼊同⽐下降 27.6% ⾄ 29.77 亿美
元，占集团收⼊的⽐重从去年同期的 37.7% 降⾄ 29% 。
美国市场则呈现出不同的经营趋势。
三季度 SHEIN 美国市场收⼊同⽐下降 9.4% ⾄ 23.69 亿美元，但履约订单量实现中个位数增⻓，需
求呈现逐⽉改善趋势。
收⼊与订单量的表现分化，主要源于美国第三⽅商城业务占⽐提升。在这⼀模式下， SHEIN 主
要确认平台服务收⼊，⽽⾮全部商品销售⾦额。
SHEIN 董事会主席兼 CEO 许仰天在业绩会上表⽰，提价会在短期内影响消费者的购买量，但只
要商品在价格、设计和质量的组合上保持竞争⼒，消费者仍会回归 SHEIN 。
许仰天预计，美国市场⻓期的常态化年增⻓率有望达到⾼个位数。
欧美之外的市场正在成为更重要的增⻓来源。
三季度， SHEIN 其他地区收⼊同⽐增⻓ 17.7% ⾄ 49.25 亿美元，占集团收⼊的⽐重已达 48% 。其

中部分拉美国家继续保持健康增⻓，中东市场的订单量也已恢复同⽐正增⻓。
收⼊承压之下， SHEIN 的盈利表现有所改善。
三季度营销费⽤同⽐下降 41.5% ⾄ 10.54 亿美元，占收⼊的⽐重从去年同期的 16.5% 降⾄
10.3% ；受部分关税退还、运费回落等因素影响，履约费⽤率也从⼆季度的 50.4% 降⾄ 45.7% 。

### Page 2

⻛险提⽰及免责条款

42 收藏 分享到 :
剔除⼀次性因素后， SHEIN 经调整净利润率仍环⽐提升⼀倍以上⾄ 4.7% 。
不过，对于欧洲市场⽽⾔，短期经营压⼒尚未完全释放。
11 ⽉ 1 ⽇起，欧盟针对每类商品额外征收 2 欧元处理费。许仰天透露，新增成本也将反映在商品
售价中，预计未来⼏个季度欧洲市场仍将承压。
为应对贸易政策变化， SHEIN 正加快推进 “ ⼩额快备 ” 模式，通过⼩批量备货及本地配送改善履约
效率。
三季度，该模式已覆盖欧洲客⼾ 40% 以上的配送量，较⼆季度增⻓⼀倍以上，预计年底将达到
约 50% ，并于 2027 年下半年覆盖当地⼤部分配送。
据许仰天介绍，相⽐跨境配送，通过 “ ⼩额快备 ” 履约的商品在配送时效、成本、转化率及单件盈
利等⽅⾯均表现更优。随着规模扩⼤，履约成本还有进⼀步下降的空间。

许仰天预计，欧洲市场或于 2027 年下半年迎来逐步复苏，⻓期年收⼊增速有望超过 10% 。
除了履约模式的调整， SHEIN 也希望触达更⼴泛的消费群体。
许仰天在业绩会上重申了三项战略优先事项：拓宽商品价格带、持续优化质量及合规⽔平，以
及加强与消费者的沟通。
在全球⽣活成本提升的背景下，许仰天认为，消费者将更加重视商品在时尚度、品质与价格上
的综合表现，这将为具备柔性供应链优势的 SHEIN 带来机会。
其中，品牌赋能业务是 SHEIN 拓宽价格带的重要抓⼿，⽬前已与 20 多个品牌展开合作。
许仰天强调，⾃营仍是 SHEIN 的核⼼业务，预计未来⾃营与第三⽅业务的 GMV 结构将趋于稳
定。在第三⽅业务内部，品牌赋能业务的增速有望快于商城业务。
“ 这件事是多年的过程，急不来。 ” 许仰天表⽰，寻找合适的品牌合作伙伴及双⽅磨合均需要时
间，但⻓期来看，品牌赋能业务可能成为关键的利润驱动⼒。
“ 希⾳的增⻓不依赖于单⼀市场。 ” 许仰天表⽰，随着业务布局进⼀步多元化， SHEIN 希望通过更

丰富的商品供给和更具竞争⼒的价值主张，满⾜全球消费者不断变化的需求。

TCT Cảng Hàng không Việt Nam (ACV) 
Áp lực lợi nhuận trong chu kỳ mở rộng công suất 
19/11/2026

KQKD 3Q2026 suy giảm do khấu hao 
và biến động tỷ giá

Lượt khách ACV dự kiến tăng chậm 
hơn toàn ngành do tác động từ Phú 
Quốc và Gia Bình

Long Thành dự kiến khai thác từ cuối 
2026, tạo áp lực lên biên lợi nhuận 
giai đoạn đầu

Duy trì khuyến nghị TRUNG LẬP, giá 
mục tiêu 39,500 VND/cổ phiếu

3Q2026, ACV ghi nhận doanh thu đạt 6,066 tỷ VND (-6% yoy) và LNST công ty mẹ 
đạt 2,206 tỷ VND (-14% yoy). Lợi nhuận suy giảm chủ yếu do (1) khấu hao tăng từ 
các nhà ga mới và (2) ghi nhận lỗ tỷ giá 168 tỷ VND trong kỳ, trái ngược với khoản lãi 
tỷ giá cùng kỳ 2025. Lũy kế 9M2026, doanh thu và LNST công ty mẹ lần lượt đạt 
19,226/7,435 tỷ VND (+0%/+2% yoy).

Chúng tôi dự phóng lượt khách quốc tế/nội địa qua hệ thống ACV tăng trưởng bình 
quân 7%/5.5%/năm đến 2030, thấp hơn toàn ngành do (1) sân bay Phú Quốc 
không còn thuộc hệ thống ACV từ đầu 2026 và (2) một phần khách quốc tế tại Nội 
Bài dịch chuyển tự nhiên sang sân bay Gia Bình khi đi vào khai thác. Mức độ dịch 
chuyển trước năm 2030 dự kiến còn hạn chế do phụ thuộc vào tiến độ hoàn thiện 
sân bay và hạ tầng giao thông kết nối.

Cảng HKQT Long Thành giai đoạn 1 dự kiến khai thác một phần từ cuối tháng 
12/2026 và bắt đầu tiếp nhận các chuyến bay quốc tế đường dài từ Tân Sơn Nhất 
vào ngày 28/03/2027, bổ sung công suất 25 triệu khách/năm cho ACV. Tuy nhiên, 
tốc độ chuyển dịch có thể chậm hơn kế hoạch do hạ tầng kết nối chưa hoàn thiện, 
trong khi chi phí khấu hao và vận hành tăng dự kiến gây áp lực lên biên lợi nhuận 
của ACV giai đoạn 2027–2028.

Sử dụng phương pháp chiết khấu dòng tiền, chúng tôi duy trì khuyến nghị TRUNG 
LẬP đối với ACV với giá mục tiêu 39,500 VND/cổ phiếu, tương đương tiềm năng tăng 
giá 4.5% so với giá đóng cửa ngày 18/11/2026.

Cơ cấu doanh thu 2025 Hoạt động kinh doanh

ACV hiện đang quản lý, đầu tư và khai thác 
hệ thống 21 Cảng hàng không trong cả nước, 
bao gồm 10 Cảng hàng không quốc tế và 11 
Cảng hàng không nội địa. Ngành nghề kinh 
doanh chính của ACV là hoạt động cung cấp 
dịch vụ hỗ trợ trực tiếp cho vận tải hàng 
không, bao gồm dịch vụ hàng không, dịch vụ 
phi hàng không và bán hàng. 
Nguồn: Báo cáo công ty, KBSV

Điểm nhấn đầu tư  Chú thích 
Tăng trưởng bền vững đến từ cả nhóm khách nội địa và quốc tế, tốc độ tăng trung bình dự kiến đạt 
5.5% và 7%/năm giai đoạn 2026-2030. 
 Chi tiết ở phần dưới của báo cáo 
Long Thành bổ sung 25 triệu khách/năm từ 2027, song khấu hao tăng mạnh khiến lợi nhuận chạm 
đáy năm 2028 trước khi phục hồi từ 2029. 
 Chi tiết ở phần dưới của báo cáo 
Quá trình lên HOSE của ACV tiến triển thuận lợi nhờ các tài sản nhà nước đang dần được tách bạch, 
tạo thuận lợi cho việc hoàn tất cổ phần hóa tài sản khu bay cho phần vốn nhà nước. 
 Tham khảo trong báo cáo kỳ trước tại đây

HOẠT ĐỘNG KINH DOANH 
KQKD 3Q2026 suy giảm do khấu 
hao và biến động tỷ giá

3Q2026, ACV ghi nhận doanh thu đạt 6,066 tỷ VND (-6% yoy) và LNST công ty 
mẹ đạt 2,206 tỷ VND (-14% yoy). Lợi nhuận suy giảm chủ yếu do (1) khấu hao 
tăng từ các nhà ga mới và (2) ghi nhận lỗ tỷ giá 168 tỷ VND trong kỳ, trái ngược 
với khoản lãi tỷ giá cùng kỳ 2025. Lũy kế 9M2026, doanh thu và LNST công ty 
mẹ lần lượt đạt 19,226/7,435 tỷ VND (+0%/+2% yoy).

Biểu đồ 6. Các quốc gia có lượng khách đến Việt Nam lớn nhất trong 10M2026 (khách) 
Trung Quốc và Hàn Quốc tiếp tục là hai thị 
trường khách quốc tế lớn nhất đến Việt Nam 
trong 10M2026 nhưng tăng trưởng hạn chế 
(+1.4% và -6.3% yoy), chủ yếu chịu tác động 
từ giá nhiên liệu tăng cao, khiến các hãng 
hàng không cắt giảm tần suất bay và du 
khách có xu hướng ưu tiên các điểm đến gần. 
Ngược lại, khách châu Âu tăng mạnh (+51.5% 
yoy), chủ yếu nhờ (1) thị trường Nga phục hồi 
(+150% yoy) sau khi các đường bay thẳng và 
charter được khôi phục, mở rộng và (2) hưởng 
lợi từ chính sách miễn thị thực bổ sung cho 12 
quốc gia châu Âu từ tháng 8/2025 và việc tăng 
cường kết nối hàng không trong năm 2026.

Nguồn: GSO

Triển vọng tăng trưởng hành khách 
tích cực, song ACV dự kiến tăng 
chậm hơn toàn ngành 
 Theo dự báo của Bộ Xây dựng, lượng hành khách thông qua các cảng hàng 
không Việt Nam dự kiến tăng trưởng bình quân 9.7%/năm giai đoạn 2026–
2030, đạt 191.2 triệu lượt vào năm 2030. Về dài hạn, Airbus dự báo lưu lượng 
hành khách hàng không Việt Nam tăng trưởng 7.7%/năm trong 20 năm tới, gần 
gấp khoảng 2 lần mức trung bình toàn cầu (3.9%/năm). Các động lực chính bao 
gồm triển vọng tăng trưởng kinh tế, nhu cầu du lịch gia tăng, chính sách miễn 
thị thực thuận lợi và việc mở rộng mạng đường bay quốc tế, cùng kế hoạch bổ 
sung đội bay của các hãng hàng không.

### Page 5

Chúng tôi dự phóng lượng khách quốc tế và nội địa của ACV tăng trưởng bình 
quân lần lượt 7% và 5.5%/năm đến 2030, thấp hơn mức tăng trưởng chung của 
toàn ngành do (1) sân bay Phú Quốc được chuyển ra khỏi hệ thống ACV từ đầu 
2026 và (2) một phần khách quốc tế tại Nội Bài dịch chuyển tự nhiên sang Gia 
Bình khi sân bay này đi vào khai thác. Chúng tôi đánh giá mức độ dịch chuyển 
trước năm 2030 còn hạn chế do phụ thuộc vào tiến độ xây dựng sân bay và hạ 
tầng kết nối. Bên cạnh đó, đề xuất chuyển các chuyến bay quốc tế từ Nội Bài 
sang Gia Bình hiện chưa được phê duyệt và chưa được phản ánh trong dự 
phóng. Nếu được thông qua, đề xuất này có thể ảnh hưởng đáng kể đến lợi 
nhuận của ACV, do lợi nhuận trên mỗi khách quốc tế cao gấp khoảng 4–5 lần 
khách nội địa, đặc biệt trong giai đoạn sau năm 2030.

Nguồn: ACV, KBSV tổng hợp  Nguồn: ACV, KBSV tổng hợp

Cảng HKQT Long Thành mở ra dư 
địa tăng trưởng dài hạn, song tạo 
áp lực lên biên lợi nhuận trong 
ngắn và trung hạn 
 Cảng HKQT Long Thành dự kiến khai thác một phần từ cuối tháng 12/2026, chủ 
yếu phục vụ các chuyến bay nội địa và một số chuyến quốc tế. Hoạt động 
chuyển các chuyến bay quốc tế đường dài và vận tải hàng hóa từ Tân Sơn Nhất 
dự kiến bắt đầu từ ngày 28/03/2027. Tuy nhiên, chúng tôi đánh giá tốc độ dịch 
chuyển có thể chậm hơn kế hoạch do phụ thuộc vào tiến độ hoàn thiện hạ tầng 
giao thông kết nối với trung tâm TP.HCM, đặc biệt là dự án mở rộng cao tốc 
TP.HCM – Long Thành – Dầu Giây và các dự án kết nối đường sắt bao gồm Thủ 
Thiêm – Long Thành.

Dự án cảng HKQT Long Thành kỳ vọng sẽ mở ra dư địa tăng trưởng dài hạn cho 
ACV, dù gây áp lực lên biên lợi nhuận trong giai đoạn đầu khai thác. Việc đưa 
Long Thành vào vận hành sẽ giúp ACV mở rộng công suất, giảm áp lực quá tải 
tại Tân Sơn Nhất, đồng thời tạo điều kiện thu hút thêm các đường bay quốc tế, 
đặc biệt là đường bay dài và hoạt động vận tải hàng hóa. Tuy nhiên, trong ngắn 
và trung hạn, chi phí khấu hao và vận hành tăng trong khi sản lượng hành 
khách cần thời gian để đạt công suất tối ưu dự kiến sẽ gây áp lực lên biên lợi 
nhuận của ACV giai đoạn 2027–2028. Trong mô hình dự phóng, chúng tôi ghi 
nhận khấu hao Long Thành theo tiến độ nghiệm thu, kết chuyển và đưa vào sử 
dụng từng hạng mục tài sản cố định, thay vì toàn bộ dự án ngay khi bắt đầu vận 
hành.

TRUNG LẬP đối với cổ phiếu ACV trong bối cảnh rủi ro từ đề xuất chuyển các 
chuyến bay quốc tế từ Nội Bài sang Gia Bình gia tăng. Giá mục tiêu của ACV là 
39,500 VND/cổ phiếu, tương đương với mức tăng giá tiềm năng 4.5% so với mức 
giá đóng cửa 37,800 VND/cổ phiếu ngày 18/11/2026. Các giả định chính bao 
gồm: 
- Giai đoạn 2026–2030, dự phóng lượt khách quốc tế/nội địa qua hệ 
thống cảng hàng không ACV tăng trưởng bình quân 7%/5.5%/năm, 
thấp hơn tốc độ tăng trưởng chung của ngành hàng không Việt Nam, 
chủ yếu do (1) ACV không còn ghi nhận sản lượng hành khách tại sân 
bay Phú Quốc và (2) giả định một phần khách quốc tế tại Nội Bài dịch 
chuyển tự nhiên sang Gia Bình với mức độ còn hạn chế do hạ tầng kết 
nối chưa hoàn thiện. Dự phóng chưa phản ánh đề xuất chuyển các 
chuyến bay quốc tế sang Gia Bình. 
- Giá dịch vụ trung bình cảng hàng không dự kiến sẽ điều chỉnh tăng 2% 
mỗi 4 năm. 
- Dự án Long Thành giai đoạn 1 và giai đoạn 2 bắt đầu đưa vào khai thác 
từ cuối 2026 và đầu 2032, gia tăng doanh thu nhưng đồng thời tạo áp 
lực lên biên lợi nhuận trong giai đoạn đầu vận hành do chi phí khấu 
hao và vận hành tăng.

Rủi ro giảm đối với giá mục tiêu ACV chủ yếu đến từ khả năng đề xuất chuyển 
các chuyến bay quốc tế từ Nội Bài sang Gia Bình được phê duyệt. Kịch bản cơ 
sở hiện chỉ phản ánh mức dịch chuyển tự nhiên. Trong trường hợp đề xuất 
được thông qua, sản lượng khách quốc tế qua hệ thống ACV có thể thấp hơn 
đáng kể so với dự phóng, kéo theo suy giảm doanh thu và lợi nhuận do khả 
năng sinh lời trên mỗi khách quốc tế cao hơn khoảng 4–5 lần so với khách nội 
địa.

## Document 442: The Global Economy’s Resilience to Shocks_ Four Observations and Three Intertwined Questions (2026-11-16)

Report source: BNP Paribas
Date: 2026-11-16

Extraction status: ok

economic-research.bnpparibas.comEcoWeek 26.42 / 16 November 2026EDITORIAL
THE GLOBAL ECONOMY’S RESILIENCE TO SHOCKS: FOUR OBSERVATIONS AND THREE INTERTWINED QUESTIONS
A review of the latest economic indicators reveals four key observations. First, the global economy continues to show re-
silience in the face of the energy shock, which has recently been compounded by the impact of rising long-term interest
rates. Second, this resilience still relies heavily on the global investment cycle, but not exclusively: household consump-

tion also provides support. Albeit on a more or less strong footing depending on the country, its contribution to growth
is non-negligible. Third, the rise in inflation remains primarily energy-driven, with the United States standing out unfa-
vorably from the Eurozone due to a more entrenched and persistent problem. Fourth, the rise in long-term government
yields is placing a significant, well-identified, yet manageable strain on public finances. Three intertwined questions arise

regarding the rise in long-term interest rates: How high can they go? Will growth continue to hold up against all odds?
How will central banks react?
FIRST OBSERVATION: THE GLOBAL ECONOMY CONTINUES TO WEATHER THE
ENERGY SHOCK WITHOUT BREAKING
The surge in energy prices following the outbreak of the Middle East
conflict in late February has had a smaller impact on economic acti -
vity than the 2022 energy shock caused by the invasion of Ukraine 1.

Although the price of a barrel of Brent crude once again (and largely so)
crossed the USD 100 threshold in October while European natural gas
prices (TTF) remain under pressure as winter approaches, the macroe-
conomic shockwave has remained much more contained than four
years ago. According to business climate surveys in the Eurozone and
the United States, the manufacturing sector has weathered the shock
remarkably well, while the impact was more visible in the services

sector and on consumer confidence before an improvement set in.
In October, the overall picture remains positive: while there are signs of
erosion, they remain contained. The PMI/ISM indices remain above the
50-point expansion threshold — clearly so in the US, and to a slightly
lesser extent in Europe. Emerging economies have also shown resi -
lience, thereby fueling global growth. Non-resident portfolio invest -
ment flows have certainly been highly volatile since the outbreak of the

conflict in Iran, but financing conditions have held up despite pressures
on energy prices and interest rates in advanced economies, and the
aggregate manufacturing PMI index for the fifteen major emerging eco-
nomies has nearly returned to its February 2026 level since September.
SECOND OBSERVATION: THE SUPPORTIVE ROLE OF HOUSEHOLD CONSUMP-
TION, IN THE SHADOW OF THE INVESTMENT CYCLE
In the current business cycle, there is much more talk of corporate in-

vestment than of household consumption. The former is indeed riding
high, driven by several mutually reinforcing trends: the AI boom, in -
creased military spending, decarbonization and electrification efforts,
and Germany’s massive infrastructure plan. As a result, this invest -
ment engine appears immune — or at least less sensitive, at the aggre-
gate level — to the energy shock than household consumption. Inco -
me and financing constraints appear to be more severe for household

consumption. This is evident in household confidence, which certainly
does not reflect the same level of optimism as business climate indica-
tors. And yet, while they show little confidence, they are quite spend -
thrift, particularly in the United States. Across the Atlantic, the current
investment cycle is certainly a major driver of growth, closely trailing
that of household consumption. However, until the second quarter of
2026, household consumption retained its role as the primary driver, as

having the largest contribution to growth (see chart). And while it was
dethroned in the third quarter according to currently available data, its
support remains very much present and substantial.
Household consumption in the Eurozone certainly does not show the
same momentum: on a quarterly average, from the first quarter of
2023 through the second quarter of 2026, it grew nearly half as fast
as in the United States (0.34% vs. 0.66%). Nevertheless, it remains the

dominant driver of growth in the Eurozone (with a contribution amoun-
ting to 0.2pp, or 71%, compared with half a percentage point for total
investment), without the same wealth effect as in the United States
and without a decline in the savings rate, which leaves it with greater
potential for improvement.
THIRD OBSERVATION: THE RISE IN INFLATION REMAINS MOSTLY ENER-
GY-DRIVEN BUT APPEARS MORE ENTRENCHED ACROSS THE ATLANTIC
On the inflation front, the rise continues to be primarily energy-driven,

even though it appears more entrenched in the United States, and ini-
tial signs of pass-through — notably via producer prices — will be
closely monitored, while overall upward pressures remain strong 2.
In the Eurozone, where harmonised inflation reached 4.0% year-on-
year in October 2026, energy accounts for nearly half of the rise in
prices. Core inflation remains contained (at 2.6% y/y), as do all alter -
native indicators, including inflation expectations. The situation in the

United States, however, calls for increased vigilance. While headline
inflation (3.7% y/y in September according to the CPI) is lower than the
